# apps/ops/services.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models import Q
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.audit.services import log_action
from apps.common.constants import (
    ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
    ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,
    ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED,
    ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED,
    ATTENDANCE_ABSENT,
    ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN,
    ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED,
    ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
    EVENT_ACTIVE,
    EVENT_COMPLETED,
    FEEDBACK_COORDINATOR_TO_VOLUNTEER,
    FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_COORDINATOR,
    FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT,
    QR_CHECKIN,
    QR_DEFAULT_TTL_MINUTES,
    QR_JOIN_EVENT,
    QR_WALKIN_REGISTRATION,
)
from apps.common.exceptions import (
    CapacityFullError,
    DomainError,
    InvalidStateError,
    OnboardingRequiredError,
    PermissionDeniedError,
)
from apps.common.permissions import (
    ensure_active_account,
    ensure_event_manager,
    is_event_manager,
)
from apps.common.security import generate_raw_token, hash_token
from apps.ops.models import (
    Assignment,
    Attendance,
    Feedback,
    OnboardingAcknowledgment,
    QrToken,
)


def check_post_eligibility(user, post) -> bool:
    """
    Check if a volunteer satisfies post requirements:
    1. If post.required_skills: user.skills must overlap post.required_skills.
    2. If post.physical_level == 'heavy': user.physical_ok and user.can_lift_heavy must be True.
    3. If post.physical_level == 'medium': user.physical_ok must be True.
    4. If post.new_volunteers_allowed is False: user.new_to_volunteer must be False.
    """
    if post.required_skills:
        user_skills = set(user.skills or [])
        required = set(post.required_skills)
        if not user_skills.intersection(required):
            return False

    if post.physical_level == "heavy":
        if not (user.physical_ok and user.can_lift_heavy):
            return False
    elif post.physical_level == "medium":
        if not user.physical_ok:
            return False

    if not post.new_volunteers_allowed and user.new_to_volunteer:
        return False

    return True


def get_remaining_post_capacity(post) -> int:
    active_count = Assignment.objects.filter(
        post=post,
        status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
    ).count()
    return max(0, post.required_headcount - active_count)


def create_assignment_from_application(application, actor=None) -> Assignment | None:
    """
    Create Assignment from an accepted EventApplication.
    - If application already has an active assignment, return it.
    - If application has a specific post, verify post eligibility and capacity.
    - If application has no post, find first eligible open post with capacity.
    - If no eligible post has capacity, return None.
    - Creates Attendance(status='expected') if missing.
    """
    existing = Assignment.objects.filter(
        application=application,
        status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
    ).first()
    if existing:
        return existing

    user = application.user
    event = application.event

    target_post = application.post
    if target_post:
        if target_post.status != "open":
            return None
        if not check_post_eligibility(user, target_post):
            return None
        if get_remaining_post_capacity(target_post) <= 0:
            return None
    else:
        # Search among event's open posts ordered by priority ascending, id ascending
        open_posts = event.posts.filter(status="open").order_by("priority", "id")
        chosen_post = None
        for post in open_posts:
            if check_post_eligibility(user, post) and get_remaining_post_capacity(post) > 0:
                chosen_post = post
                break
        if not chosen_post:
            return None
        target_post = chosen_post

    assigned_by = "auto" if actor is None else ("admin" if getattr(actor, "is_admin", False) else "coordinator")
    assigned_by_user = None if assigned_by == "auto" else actor

    with transaction.atomic():
        assignment = Assignment.objects.create(
            event=event,
            post=target_post,
            user=user,
            application=application,
            status=ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,
            assigned_by=assigned_by,
            assigned_by_user=assigned_by_user,
            schedule_start=event.start_at,
            schedule_end=event.end_at,
            conditions_snapshot=application.conditions_snapshot or {},
        )

        Attendance.objects.get_or_create(
            event=event,
            user=user,
            defaults={
                "assignment": assignment,
                "status": ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
            },
        )

        log_action(
            actor=actor or user,
            action="create_assignment",
            entity_type="assignment",
            entity_id=assignment.id,
            after_data={"post_id": target_post.id if target_post else None, "user_id": user.id},
        )

    return assignment


def generate_qr_token(
    actor,
    event,
    purpose: str,
    max_uses: int = None,
    ttl_minutes: int = 240,
) -> tuple[QrToken, str]:
    """
    Generate QR Token for an event.
    Stores token_hash (SHA-256). Raw token is returned and never persisted.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    valid_purposes = {QR_WALKIN_REGISTRATION, QR_CHECKIN, QR_JOIN_EVENT}
    if purpose not in valid_purposes:
        raise DomainError(f"Invalid QR token purpose: {purpose}", code="invalid_purpose")

    if max_uses is not None and max_uses <= 0:
        raise DomainError("max_uses must be greater than 0.", code="invalid_max_uses")

    raw_token = generate_raw_token()
    token_hash = hash_token(raw_token)

    ttl = ttl_minutes if ttl_minutes is not None else QR_DEFAULT_TTL_MINUTES
    expires_at = timezone.now() + timedelta(minutes=ttl)

    with transaction.atomic():
        qr_token = QrToken.objects.create(
            event=event,
            created_by=actor,
            purpose=purpose,
            token_hash=token_hash,
            max_uses=max_uses,
            expires_at=expires_at,
        )

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="generate_qr_token",
            entity_type="qr_token",
            entity_id=qr_token.id,
            after_data={"purpose": purpose, "max_uses": max_uses, "expires_at": str(expires_at)},
        )

    return qr_token, raw_token


def resolve_qr_token(raw_token: str) -> dict:
    """
    Resolve QR token metadata without consuming it.
    """
    token_hash = hash_token(raw_token)
    qr_token = QrToken.objects.select_related("event").filter(token_hash=token_hash).first()

    if not qr_token:
        raise DomainError("Invalid QR token.", code="invalid_token")

    if qr_token.revoked_at is not None:
        raise DomainError("QR token has been revoked.", code="revoked_token")

    if qr_token.expires_at <= timezone.now():
        raise DomainError("QR token has expired.", code="expired_token")

    if qr_token.max_uses is not None and qr_token.used_count >= qr_token.max_uses:
        raise CapacityFullError("QR token usage limit reached.", code="qr_limit_reached")

    return {
        "event_id": qr_token.event_id,
        "title": qr_token.event.title,
        "purpose": qr_token.purpose,
        "expires_at": qr_token.expires_at,
        "used_count": qr_token.used_count,
        "max_uses": qr_token.max_uses,
    }


def redeem_qr_token(user, raw_token: str) -> dict:
    """
    Safely redeem a QR token within a transaction lock.
    Dispatches according to purpose:
    - walkin_registration -> apply_to_event(source='qr_walkin')
    - checkin -> check_in(method='qr_scan')
    - join_event -> apply_to_event(source='invite')
    """
    ensure_active_account(user)

    token_hash = hash_token(raw_token)

    with transaction.atomic():
        qr_token = (
            QrToken.objects.select_for_update()
            .select_related("event")
            .filter(token_hash=token_hash)
            .first()
        )

        if not qr_token:
            raise DomainError("Invalid QR token.", code="invalid_token")

        if qr_token.revoked_at is not None:
            raise DomainError("QR token has been revoked.", code="revoked_token")

        if qr_token.expires_at <= timezone.now():
            raise DomainError("QR token has expired.", code="expired_token")

        if qr_token.max_uses is not None and qr_token.used_count >= qr_token.max_uses:
            raise CapacityFullError("QR token usage limit reached.", code="qr_limit_reached")

        qr_token.used_count += 1
        qr_token.save(update_fields=["used_count"])

        # Dispatch by purpose
        if qr_token.purpose == QR_WALKIN_REGISTRATION:
            from apps.events.services import apply_to_event
            app = apply_to_event(user, qr_token.event, source="qr_walkin", qr_token=qr_token)
            result = {
                "action": QR_WALKIN_REGISTRATION,
                "application_id": app.id,
                "status": app.status,
            }
        elif qr_token.purpose == QR_CHECKIN:
            att = check_in(
                actor=user,
                event=qr_token.event,
                user=user,
                method="qr_scan",
                qr_token=qr_token,
            )
            result = {
                "action": QR_CHECKIN,
                "status": "checked_in",
                "attendance_id": att.id,
                "checkin_at": att.checkin_at,
            }
        elif qr_token.purpose == QR_JOIN_EVENT:
            from apps.events.services import apply_to_event
            app = apply_to_event(user, qr_token.event, source="invite")
            result = {
                "action": QR_JOIN_EVENT,
                "application_id": app.id,
                "status": app.status,
            }
        else:
            raise DomainError(f"Unsupported purpose: {qr_token.purpose}", code="invalid_purpose")

        log_action(
            actor=user,
            action="redeem_qr_token",
            entity_type="qr_token",
            entity_id=qr_token.id,
            after_data={"purpose": qr_token.purpose, "used_count": qr_token.used_count},
        )

    return result


def acknowledge_onboarding(user, event, method: str = "app_tick") -> OnboardingAcknowledgment:
    """
    Record onboarding acknowledgment for current event onboarding_version.
    """
    ensure_active_account(user)

    with transaction.atomic():
        ack, created = OnboardingAcknowledgment.objects.update_or_create(
            event=event,
            user=user,
            onboarding_version=event.onboarding_version,
            defaults={
                "method": method,
                "acknowledged_at": timezone.now(),
            },
        )

        log_action(
            actor=user,
            action="acknowledge_onboarding",
            entity_type="onboarding_ack",
            entity_id=ack.id,
            after_data={"event_id": event.id, "version": event.onboarding_version, "method": method},
        )

    return ack


def check_in(
    actor,
    event,
    user=None,
    method: str = "qr_scan",
    qr_token=None,
    target_user=None,
) -> Attendance:
    """
    Check-in volunteer to an active event.
    Returns Attendance instance. Idempotent if already checked in.
    """
    ensure_active_account(actor)
    target = user or target_user
    if not target:
        raise DomainError("Target volunteer is required.", code="user_required")

    if event.status != EVENT_ACTIVE:
        raise InvalidStateError(
            f"Event must be active to check in. Current status: '{event.status}'."
        )

    assignment = Assignment.objects.filter(
        event=event,
        user=target,
        status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
    ).first()
    if not assignment:
        raise DomainError(
            "User does not have an active assignment for this event.",
            code="no_active_assignment",
        )

    if event.require_onboarding_ack:
        has_ack = OnboardingAcknowledgment.objects.filter(
            event=event,
            user=target,
            onboarding_version=event.onboarding_version,
        ).exists()
        if not has_ack:
            raise OnboardingRequiredError(
                "Volunteer must acknowledge onboarding requirements before checking in."
            )

    if actor.id == target.id:
        if method != "qr_scan":
            raise DomainError("Volunteers can only check in via QR scan.", code="invalid_checkin_method")
        if not qr_token or qr_token.purpose != QR_CHECKIN:
            raise DomainError("Valid check-in QR token is required.", code="invalid_qr_token")
    else:
        ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    attendance = Attendance.objects.filter(event=event, user=target).first()
    if attendance and attendance.status == ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN:
        return attendance

    now = timezone.now()
    with transaction.atomic():
        if not attendance:
            attendance = Attendance.objects.create(
                event=event,
                user=target,
                assignment=assignment,
                qr_token=qr_token,
                status=ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN,
                checkin_at=now,
                method=method,
                recorded_by=actor,
            )
        else:
            attendance.assignment = assignment
            attendance.qr_token = qr_token
            attendance.status = ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN
            attendance.checkin_at = now
            attendance.method = method
            attendance.recorded_by = actor
            attendance.save()

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="check_in",
            entity_type="attendance",
            entity_id=attendance.id,
            after_data={"method": method, "target_user_id": target.id},
        )

    return attendance


def check_out(actor, event, user=None, target_user=None) -> Attendance:
    """
    Check-out volunteer from an active event.
    """
    ensure_active_account(actor)
    target = user or target_user
    if not target:
        raise DomainError("Target volunteer is required.", code="user_required")

    if actor.id != target.id:
        ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    attendance = Attendance.objects.filter(event=event, user=target).first()
    if not attendance or attendance.status != ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN:
        raise InvalidStateError("Volunteer is not currently checked in.", code="not_checked_in")

    with transaction.atomic():
        attendance.checkout_at = timezone.now()
        attendance.save(update_fields=["checkout_at"])

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="check_out",
            entity_type="attendance",
            entity_id=attendance.id,
            after_data={"checkout_at": str(attendance.checkout_at), "target_user_id": target.id},
        )

    return attendance


def mark_absent(actor, event, user=None, target_user=None) -> Attendance:
    """
    Coordinator or Admin marks volunteer absent.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, event)
    target = user or target_user
    if not target:
        raise DomainError("Target volunteer is required.", code="user_required")

    attendance = Attendance.objects.filter(event=event, user=target).first()
    if attendance and attendance.status in (ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN, ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED):
        raise InvalidStateError(
            f"Cannot mark absent because attendance is already '{attendance.status}'.",
            code="invalid_state",
        )

    assignment = Assignment.objects.filter(
        event=event,
        user=target,
        status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
    ).first()

    with transaction.atomic():
        if not attendance:
            attendance = Attendance.objects.create(
                event=event,
                user=target,
                assignment=assignment,
                status=ATTENDANCE_ABSENT,
                recorded_by=actor,
            )
        else:
            attendance.status = ATTENDANCE_ABSENT
            attendance.recorded_by = actor
            attendance.save(update_fields=["status", "recorded_by"])

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="mark_absent",
            entity_type="attendance",
            entity_id=attendance.id,
            after_data={"target_user_id": target.id},
        )

    return attendance


def submit_feedback(
    user,
    event,
    direction: str,
    target_user=None,
    score: int = None,
    tags=None,
    comment: str = "",
) -> Feedback:
    """
    Submit feedback between coordinator and volunteer or for the event.
    Requirements:
    - Event must be completed.
    - Score must be between 1 and 5.
    - If coordinator_to_volunteer: rater is coordinator/admin, target_user required and had active assignment.
    - If volunteer_to_event: rater had active assignment, target_user must be None.
    - If volunteer_to_coordinator: rater had active assignment, target_user forced to event.coordinator.
    """
    ensure_active_account(user)

    if event.status != EVENT_COMPLETED:
        raise InvalidStateError(
            "Feedback can only be submitted for completed events.",
            code="event_not_completed",
        )

    if score is None or not (1 <= int(score) <= 5):
        raise DomainError("Feedback score is required and must be between 1 and 5.", code="invalid_score")

    valid_directions = {
        FEEDBACK_COORDINATOR_TO_VOLUNTEER,
        FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT,
        FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_COORDINATOR,
    }
    if direction not in valid_directions:
        raise DomainError(f"Invalid feedback direction: {direction}", code="invalid_direction")

    final_target = target_user

    if direction == FEEDBACK_COORDINATOR_TO_VOLUNTEER:
        if not is_event_manager(user, event):
            raise PermissionDeniedError("Only event coordinators or admins can rate volunteers.")
        if not final_target:
            raise DomainError("Target volunteer is required.", code="target_user_required")
        had_assignment = Assignment.objects.filter(
            event=event,
            user=final_target,
        ).exclude(status=ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED).exists()
        if not had_assignment:
            raise DomainError("Target volunteer had no assignment for this event.", code="invalid_target_user")

    elif direction == FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT:
        had_assignment = Assignment.objects.filter(
            event=event,
            user=user,
        ).exclude(status=ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED).exists()
        if not had_assignment:
            raise PermissionDeniedError("Only participating volunteers can submit event feedback.")
        final_target = None

    elif direction == FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_COORDINATOR:
        had_assignment = Assignment.objects.filter(
            event=event,
            user=user,
        ).exclude(status=ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED).exists()
        if not had_assignment:
            raise PermissionDeniedError("Only participating volunteers can submit coordinator feedback.")
        final_target = event.coordinator

    # Check duplicate feedback
    if final_target is None:
        if Feedback.objects.filter(event=event, rater_user=user, direction=direction, target_user__isnull=True).exists():
            raise DomainError("Feedback has already been submitted for this event.", code="duplicate_feedback")
    else:
        if Feedback.objects.filter(event=event, rater_user=user, direction=direction, target_user=final_target).exists():
            raise DomainError("Feedback has already been submitted for this user.", code="duplicate_feedback")

    with transaction.atomic():
        feedback = Feedback.objects.create(
            event=event,
            rater_user=user,
            target_user=final_target,
            direction=direction,
            score=int(score),
            tags=tags or [],
            comment=comment or "",
            status="active",
        )

        log_action(
            actor=user,
            action="submit_feedback",
            entity_type="feedback",
            entity_id=feedback.id,
            after_data={
                "direction": direction,
                "score": int(score),
                "target_user_id": final_target.id if final_target else None,
            },
        )

    return feedback


def run_auto_assignment(actor, event) -> list[Assignment]:
    """
    Coordinator or Admin runs automated assignment algorithm.
    1. Lock event.
    2. Get accepted applications without active assignment.
    3. For each application, call create_assignment_from_application.
    4. Build groups: group by post, group size = event.group_size. Code format "G1", "G2" per post.
    5. Leader assignment: if post.leader_needed or event.leader_strategy in ("first_experienced", "experienced_preferred"),
       choose first member with has_leader_experience=True. Ensure only one leader per group.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    from apps.events.models import Event, EventApplication, EventPost

    created_assignments = []

    with transaction.atomic():
        locked_event = Event.objects.select_for_update().get(id=event.id)

        # Get accepted applications without active assignment
        unassigned_apps = (
            EventApplication.objects.select_for_update()
            .filter(event=locked_event, status="accepted")
            .filter(~Q(assignments__status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES))
            .distinct()
            .order_by("applied_at", "id")
        )

        for app in unassigned_apps:
            assignment = create_assignment_from_application(app, actor=None)
            if assignment:
                created_assignments.append(assignment)

        # Group organization and leader assignment
        group_size = locked_event.group_size
        posts = locked_event.posts.all().order_by("priority", "id")

        group_seq = 1
        for post in posts:
            post_assignments = list(
                Assignment.objects.select_for_update()
                .filter(event=locked_event, post=post, status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES)
                .select_related("user")
                .order_by("id")
            )

            if not post_assignments:
                continue

            chunk_size = group_size if (group_size and group_size > 0) else len(post_assignments)

            for i in range(0, len(post_assignments), chunk_size):
                chunk = post_assignments[i : i + chunk_size]
                group_code = f"G{group_seq}"
                group_seq += 1

                # Check if group already has an active leader
                has_existing_leader = any(a.is_group_leader for a in chunk)
                leader_assigned = has_existing_leader

                should_assign_leader = post.leader_needed or locked_event.leader_strategy in (
                    "first_experienced",
                    "experienced_preferred",
                )

                for a in chunk:
                    a.group_code = group_code
                    if should_assign_leader and not leader_assigned and a.user.has_leader_experience:
                        a.is_group_leader = True
                        leader_assigned = True
                    elif not has_existing_leader and not a.is_group_leader:
                        a.is_group_leader = False
                    a.save(update_fields=["group_code", "is_group_leader"])

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="run_auto_assignment",
            entity_type="event",
            entity_id=locked_event.id,
            after_data={"assignments_created": len(created_assignments)},
        )

    return created_assignments


def assign_manually(
    actor,
    application,
    post=None,
    group_code=None,
    is_group_leader: bool = False,
) -> Assignment:
    """
    Coordinator or Admin manually assigns or updates volunteer assignment.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, application.event)

    target_post = post or application.post

    with transaction.atomic():
        if application.status != "accepted":
            application.status = "accepted"
            application.decided_by = actor
            application.decided_at = timezone.now()
            if target_post:
                application.post = target_post
            application.save()

        # Check existing active assignment
        assignment = Assignment.objects.filter(
            application=application,
            status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
        ).first()

        if is_group_leader and not group_code:
            raise DomainError(
                "Group leader must have a group code assigned.",
                code="group_code_required",
            )

        if is_group_leader and group_code:
            existing_leader = (
                Assignment.objects.filter(
                    event=application.event,
                    group_code=group_code,
                    is_group_leader=True,
                )
                .exclude(id=assignment.id if assignment else None)
                .filter(status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES)
                .exists()
            )
            if existing_leader:
                raise DomainError(
                    f"Group '{group_code}' already has an active leader.",
                    code="duplicate_group_leader",
                )

        assigned_by = "admin" if getattr(actor, "is_admin", False) else "coordinator"

        if not assignment:
            assignment = Assignment.objects.create(
                event=application.event,
                post=target_post,
                user=application.user,
                application=application,
                status=ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,
                assigned_by=assigned_by,
                assigned_by_user=actor,
                group_code=group_code,
                is_group_leader=is_group_leader,
                schedule_start=application.event.start_at,
                schedule_end=application.event.end_at,
                conditions_snapshot=application.conditions_snapshot or {},
            )
            Attendance.objects.get_or_create(
                event=application.event,
                user=application.user,
                defaults={
                    "assignment": assignment,
                    "status": ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
                },
            )
        else:
            assignment.post = target_post
            assignment.group_code = group_code
            assignment.is_group_leader = is_group_leader
            assignment.assigned_by = assigned_by
            assignment.assigned_by_user = actor
            assignment.save()

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="assign_manually",
            entity_type="assignment",
            entity_id=assignment.id,
            after_data={"post_id": target_post.id if target_post else None, "group_code": group_code},
        )

    return assignment


def confirm_assignment(user, assignment: Assignment) -> Assignment:
    """
    Volunteer confirms own assignment.
    """
    if assignment.user_id != user.id:
        raise PermissionDeniedError("Cannot confirm another volunteer's assignment.")

    if assignment.status not in (ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,):
        if assignment.status == ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED:
            return assignment
        raise InvalidStateError(f"Cannot confirm assignment in status '{assignment.status}'.")

    with transaction.atomic():
        assignment.status = ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED
        assignment.save(update_fields=["status"])

        log_action(
            actor=user,
            action="confirm_assignment",
            entity_type="assignment",
            entity_id=assignment.id,
        )

    return assignment


def cancel_assignment(actor, assignment: Assignment, reason: str = "") -> Assignment:
    """
    Coordinator, Admin, or Volunteer cancels an assignment.
    """
    if assignment.user_id != actor.id:
        ensure_event_manager(actor, assignment.event)

    if assignment.status == ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED:
        return assignment

    with transaction.atomic():
        assignment.status = ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED
        assignment.save(update_fields=["status"])

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="cancel_assignment",
            entity_type="assignment",
            entity_id=assignment.id,
            after_data={"reason": reason},
        )

    return assignment

# apps/ops/views.py
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404
from rest_framework import generics, status
from rest_framework.permissions import AllowAny, IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.accounts.models import User
from apps.common.security import hash_token
from apps.events.models import Event, EventApplication, EventPost
from apps.events.serializers import EventApplicationSerializer
from apps.ops.models import Assignment, Attendance, Feedback, QrToken
from apps.ops.serializers import (
    AssignmentCancelSerializer,
    AssignmentSerializer,
    AttendanceSerializer,
    CheckInSerializer,
    CheckOutSerializer,
    FeedbackCreateSerializer,
    FeedbackSerializer,
    ManualAssignmentSerializer,
    MarkAbsentSerializer,
    OnboardingAcknowledgeSerializer,
    OnboardingAcknowledgmentSerializer,
    QrTokenGenerateSerializer,
    QrTokenRedeemSerializer,
    QrTokenResolveSerializer,
    QrTokenSerializer,
)
from apps.ops.services import (
    acknowledge_onboarding,
    assign_manually,
    cancel_assignment,
    check_in,
    check_out,
    confirm_assignment,
    generate_qr_token,
    mark_absent,
    redeem_qr_token,
    resolve_qr_token,
    run_auto_assignment,
    submit_feedback,
)


class EventQrGenerateView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        serializer = QrTokenGenerateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        qr_token, raw_token = generate_qr_token(
            actor=request.user,
            event=event,
            purpose=serializer.validated_data["purpose"],
            max_uses=serializer.validated_data.get("max_uses"),
            ttl_minutes=serializer.validated_data.get("ttl_minutes", 240),
        )

        return Response(
            {
                "raw_token": raw_token,
                "token": raw_token,
                "qr_token": QrTokenSerializer(qr_token).data,
            },
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )


class QrResolveView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]

    def post(self, request):
        serializer = QrTokenResolveSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        preview = resolve_qr_token(raw_token=serializer.validated_data["token"])
        return Response(preview, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class QrRedeemView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request):
        serializer = QrTokenRedeemSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        res = redeem_qr_token(user=request.user, raw_token=serializer.validated_data["token"])
        return Response(res, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EventAutoRunAssignmentView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        assignments = run_auto_assignment(actor=request.user, event=event)
        return Response(
            AssignmentSerializer(assignments, many=True).data,
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        )


class EventManualAssignmentView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        serializer = ManualAssignmentSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        application = get_object_or_404(
            EventApplication,
            pk=serializer.validated_data["application_id"],
            event=event,
        )

        post = None
        if serializer.validated_data.get("post_id"):
            post = get_object_or_404(
                EventPost,
                pk=serializer.validated_data["post_id"],
                event=event,
            )

        assignment = assign_manually(
            actor=request.user,
            application=application,
            post=post,
            group_code=serializer.validated_data.get("group_code"),
            is_group_leader=serializer.validated_data.get("is_group_leader", False),
        )

        return Response(AssignmentSerializer(assignment).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class AssignmentConfirmView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        assignment = get_object_or_404(Assignment, pk=pk)
        updated = confirm_assignment(user=request.user, assignment=assignment)
        return Response(AssignmentSerializer(updated).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class AssignmentCancelView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        assignment = get_object_or_404(Assignment, pk=pk)
        serializer = AssignmentCancelSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        cancelled = cancel_assignment(
            actor=request.user,
            assignment=assignment,
            reason=serializer.validated_data.get("reason", ""),
        )
        return Response(AssignmentSerializer(cancelled).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EventCheckInView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        serializer = CheckInSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        target_user = request.user
        if serializer.validated_data.get("user_id"):
            target_user = get_object_or_404(User, pk=serializer.validated_data["user_id"])

        qr_token = None
        raw_token = serializer.validated_data.get("token")
        if raw_token:
            qr_token = QrToken.objects.filter(token_hash=hash_token(raw_token)).first()

        attendance = check_in(
            actor=request.user,
            event=event,
            user=target_user,
            method=serializer.validated_data.get("method", "qr_scan"),
            qr_token=qr_token,
        )
        return Response(AttendanceSerializer(attendance).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EventCheckOutView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        serializer = CheckOutSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        target_user = request.user
        if serializer.validated_data.get("user_id"):
            target_user = get_object_or_404(User, pk=serializer.validated_data["user_id"])

        attendance = check_out(actor=request.user, event=event, user=target_user)
        return Response(AttendanceSerializer(attendance).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EventMarkAbsentView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        serializer = MarkAbsentSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        target_user = get_object_or_404(User, pk=serializer.validated_data["user_id"])
        attendance = mark_absent(actor=request.user, event=event, user=target_user)
        return Response(AttendanceSerializer(attendance).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EventOnboardingAcknowledgeView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        serializer = OnboardingAcknowledgeSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        ack = acknowledge_onboarding(
            user=request.user,
            event=event,
            method=serializer.validated_data.get("method", "app_tick"),
        )
        return Response(OnboardingAcknowledgmentSerializer(ack).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EventFeedbackView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        serializer = FeedbackCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        target_user = None
        if serializer.validated_data.get("target_user_id"):
            target_user = get_object_or_404(User, pk=serializer.validated_data["target_user_id"])

        feedback = submit_feedback(
            user=request.user,
            event=event,
            direction=serializer.validated_data["direction"],
            target_user=target_user,
            score=serializer.validated_data["score"],
            tags=serializer.validated_data.get("tags", []),
            comment=serializer.validated_data.get("comment", ""),
        )
        return Response(FeedbackSerializer(feedback).data, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class MeApplicationsListView(generics.ListAPIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    serializer_class = EventApplicationSerializer

    def get_queryset(self):
        if getattr(self, "swagger_fake_view", False) or not getattr(self.request.user, "is_authenticated", False):
            return EventApplication.objects.none()
        return (
            EventApplication.objects.filter(user=self.request.user)
            .select_related("event", "post")
            .order_by("-applied_at")
        )


class MeAssignmentsListView(generics.ListAPIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    serializer_class = AssignmentSerializer

    def get_queryset(self):
        if getattr(self, "swagger_fake_view", False) or not getattr(self.request.user, "is_authenticated", False):
            return Assignment.objects.none()
        return (
            Assignment.objects.filter(user=self.request.user)
            .select_related("event", "post", "user")
            .order_by("-created_at")
        )

from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand
from apps.events.models import CharityField

INITIAL_CHARITY_FIELDS = [
    ('teaching', 'Teaching', 10),
    ('food_distribution', 'Food Distribution', 20),
    ('elderly_care', 'Elderly Care', 30),
    ('child_care', 'Child Care', 40),
    ('animal_welfare', 'Animal Welfare', 50),
    ('environment', 'Environment', 60),
    ('disaster_support', 'Disaster Support', 70),
    ('blood_donation', 'Blood Donation', 80),
    ('fundraising', 'Fundraising', 90),
    ('logistics', 'Logistics', 100),
    ('cleaning', 'Cleaning', 110),
    ('cooking', 'Cooking', 120),
    ('other', 'Other', 999),
]


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = 'Seeds initial charity fields from volunteer_coordination_system_db_v0_5.sql'

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        created_count = 0
        updated_count = 0

        for code, label, sort_order in INITIAL_CHARITY_FIELDS:
            obj, created = CharityField.objects.update_or_create(
                code=code,
                defaults={'label': label, 'sort_order': sort_order, 'active': True}
            )
            if created:
                created_count += 1
            else:
                updated_count += 1

        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                f'Successfully seeded charity fields. Created: {created_count}, Updated: {updated_count}'
            )
        )

# apps/events/management/commands/seed_demo_data.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import CoordinatorProfile, User
from apps.common.constants import (
    APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
    ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED,
    ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
    COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
    EVENT_PUBLISHED,
    VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
)
from apps.common.security import compute_phone_hash
from apps.events.models import CharityField, Event, EventApplication, EventCharityField, EventPost
from apps.ops.models import Assignment, Attendance


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Seeds demo users, coordinator, event, posts, and confirmed assignment (idempotent)."

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        now = timezone.now()

        # 1. Admin User
        admin_phone = "+959111111111"
        admin_hash = compute_phone_hash(admin_phone)
        admin_user, _ = User.objects.update_or_create(
            phone_hash=admin_hash,
            defaults={
                "display_name": "Demo Admin",
                "is_admin": True,
                "is_staff": True,
                "is_superuser": True,
                "account_status": "active",
                "verification_status": VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
            },
        )
        admin_user.set_password("admin123")
        admin_user.save(update_fields=["password"])

        # Superadmin credentials for Django Admin panel login
        superadmin_user, _ = User.objects.update_or_create(
            phone_hash="12345",
            defaults={
                "display_name": "admin",
                "is_admin": True,
                "is_staff": True,
                "is_superuser": True,
                "account_status": "active",
                "verification_status": VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
            },
        )
        superadmin_user.set_password("12345")
        superadmin_user.save(update_fields=["password"])

        # 2. Approved Coordinator
        coord_phone = "+959222222222"
        coord_hash = compute_phone_hash(coord_phone)
        coord_user, _ = User.objects.update_or_create(
            phone_hash=coord_hash,
            defaults={
                "display_name": "Demo Coordinator",
                "account_status": "active",
                "verification_status": VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
            },
        )
        CoordinatorProfile.objects.update_or_create(
            user=coord_user,
            defaults={
                "status": COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
                "approved_by": admin_user,
                "approved_at": now,
                "notes": "Demo coordinator approved profile.",
            },
        )

        # 3. Volunteers
        vol1_phone = "+959333333333"
        vol1_hash = compute_phone_hash(vol1_phone)
        vol1_user, _ = User.objects.update_or_create(
            phone_hash=vol1_hash,
            defaults={
                "display_name": "Demo Volunteer Alpha",
                "account_status": "active",
                "skills": ["teaching", "logistics"],
                "has_leader_experience": True,
                "physical_ok": True,
                "can_lift_heavy": True,
                "new_to_volunteer": False,
            },
        )

        vol2_phone = "+959444444444"
        vol2_hash = compute_phone_hash(vol2_phone)
        vol2_user, _ = User.objects.update_or_create(
            phone_hash=vol2_hash,
            defaults={
                "display_name": "Demo Volunteer Beta",
                "account_status": "active",
                "skills": ["cooking"],
                "has_leader_experience": False,
                "physical_ok": True,
                "can_lift_heavy": False,
                "new_to_volunteer": True,
            },
        )

        # 4. Published Event
        event, _ = Event.objects.update_or_create(
            coordinator=coord_user,
            title="Yangon Community Relief Drive",
            defaults={
                "description": "Annual food and education relief program for Yangon river communities.",
                "purpose_type": "charity_event",
                "status": EVENT_PUBLISHED,
                "location_name": "Yangon Riverside Center",
                "start_at": now + timedelta(days=5),
                "end_at": now + timedelta(days=5, hours=8),
                "application_deadline": now + timedelta(days=4),
                "acceptance_mode": "auto",
                "max_participants": 30,
                "group_size": 5,
                "require_onboarding_ack": True,
                "onboarding_text": "Please bring photo ID, water bottle, and safety footwear.",
                "onboarding_version": 1,
            },
        )

        # Link charity fields
        logistics_field = CharityField.objects.filter(code="logistics").first()
        teaching_field = CharityField.objects.filter(code="teaching").first()
        if logistics_field:
            EventCharityField.objects.get_or_create(event=event, field_code=logistics_field)
        if teaching_field:
            EventCharityField.objects.get_or_create(event=event, field_code=teaching_field)

        # 5. Three Posts with distinct criteria
        post1, _ = EventPost.objects.update_or_create(
            event=event,
            title="Logistics & Heavy Distribution",
            defaults={
                "description": "Loading trucks and carrying food supply packages.",
                "required_headcount": 8,
                "physical_level": "heavy",
                "required_skills": ["logistics"],
                "leader_needed": True,
                "status": "open",
                "priority": 1,
            },
        )

        post2, _ = EventPost.objects.update_or_create(
            event=event,
            title="Youth Workshop Facilitator",
            defaults={
                "description": "Leading community education and skills sessions.",
                "required_headcount": 6,
                "physical_level": "light",
                "required_skills": ["teaching"],
                "leader_needed": True,
                "status": "open",
                "priority": 2,
            },
        )

        post3, _ = EventPost.objects.update_or_create(
            event=event,
            title="Community Kitchen Helper",
            defaults={
                "description": "Food preparation and general helper duties.",
                "required_headcount": 10,
                "physical_level": "medium",
                "required_skills": ["cooking"],
                "new_volunteers_allowed": True,
                "status": "open",
                "priority": 3,
            },
        )

        # 6. Accepted Application & Confirmed Assignment for Volunteer 1
        application, _ = EventApplication.objects.update_or_create(
            event=event,
            user=vol1_user,
            defaults={
                "post": post1,
                "source": "browse",
                "status": APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
                "decided_by": coord_user,
                "decided_at": now,
                "conditions_snapshot": {
                    "meals_provided": event.meals_provided,
                    "lodging_provided": event.lodging_provided,
                },
            },
        )

        assignment, _ = Assignment.objects.update_or_create(
            event=event,
            user=vol1_user,
            defaults={
                "application": application,
                "post": post1,
                "status": ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED,
                "assigned_by": "coordinator",
                "assigned_by_user": coord_user,
                "group_code": "G1",
                "is_group_leader": True,
                "schedule_start": event.start_at,
                "schedule_end": event.end_at,
                "conditions_snapshot": application.conditions_snapshot or {},
            },
        )

        Attendance.objects.update_or_create(
            event=event,
            user=vol1_user,
            defaults={
                "assignment": assignment,
                "status": ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
            },
        )

        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                f"Successfully seeded demo data: 1 admin ({admin_user.display_name}), "
                f"1 coordinator ({coord_user.display_name}), 2 volunteers, "
                f"1 event ('{event.title}') with 3 posts, 1 confirmed assignment."
            )
        )

            model_name='qrtoken',
            constraint=models.CheckConstraint(condition=models.Q(('max_uses__isnull', True), ('used_count__lte', models.F('max_uses')), _connector='OR'), name='ck_qr_used_count'),
        ),
        migrations.AddIndex(
            model_name='attendance',
            index=models.Index(fields=['event', 'status'], name='idx_attend_event_status'),
        ),
        migrations.AddConstraint(
            model_name='attendance',
            constraint=models.UniqueConstraint(fields=('event', 'user'), name='uq_attendance_event_user'),
        ),
        migrations.AddConstraint(
            model_name='attendance',
            constraint=models.CheckConstraint(condition=models.Q(models.Q(('status__in', ['checked_in', 'completed']), _negated=True), ('checkin_at__isnull', False), _connector='OR'), name='ck_attendance_checkin'),
        ),
        migrations.AddConstraint(
            model_name='attendance',
            constraint=models.CheckConstraint(condition=models.Q(('checkout_at__isnull', True), models.Q(('checkin_at__isnull', False), ('checkout_at__gte', models.F('checkin_at'))), _connector='OR'), name='ck_attendance_checkout'),
        ),
    ]

from django.contrib import admin

class CoordinatorProfileInline(admin.StackedInline):
    model = CoordinatorProfile
    fk_name = "user"
    extra = 0
    readonly_fields = ("approved_by", "approved_at", "created_at")


class UserCharityInterestInline(admin.TabularInline):
    model = UserCharityInterest
    extra = 0
    readonly_fields = ("created_at",)


class UserVerificationImageInline(admin.TabularInline):
    model = UserVerificationImage
    fk_name = "user"
    extra = 0
    readonly_fields = (
        "image_type",
        "status",
        "comparison_status",
        "comparison_score",
        "is_current",
        "reviewed_by",
        "reviewed_at",
        "created_at",

@admin.register(User)
class UserAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = (
        "id",
        "display_name",
        "phone_hash",
        "account_status",
        "verification_status",
        "is_admin",
        "is_staff",
    )
    list_filter = ("account_status", "verification_status", "is_admin", "is_staff", "blood_type")
    search_fields = ("display_name", "phone_hash", "home_area")
    ordering = ("-created_at",)
    inlines = [CoordinatorProfileInline, UserCharityInterestInline, UserVerificationImageInline]
    actions = ["approve_selected_coordinators"]

    @admin.action(description="Approve coordinator profile for selected users")
    def approve_selected_coordinators(self, request, queryset):
        for user in queryset:
            profile = getattr(user, "coordinator_profile", None)
            if profile and profile.status != "approved":
                try:
                    approve_coordinator(actor=request.user, profile=profile)
                except Exception as e:
                    self.message_user(request, f"Error approving coordinator for {user}: {e}", level="error")


@admin.register(CoordinatorProfile)
class CoordinatorProfileAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("user", "status", "approved_by", "approved_at", "created_at")
    list_filter = ("status",)
    search_fields = ("user__display_name", "user__phone_hash", "notes")
    actions = ["approve_selected_coordinators"]

    @admin.action(description="Approve selected coordinators")
    def approve_selected_coordinators(self, request, queryset):
        for profile in queryset:
            if profile.status != "approved":
                try:
                    approve_coordinator(actor=request.user, profile=profile)
                except Exception as e:
                    self.message_user(request, f"Error approving coordinator {profile.user}: {e}", level="error")

@admin.register(UserVerificationImage)
class UserVerificationImageAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = (
        "id",
        "user",
        "image_type",
        "status",
        "comparison_status",
        "comparison_score",
        "is_current",
        "reviewed_by",
        "inspect_image",
        "created_at",
    )
    list_filter = ("status", "comparison_status", "image_type", "is_current")
    search_fields = ("user__display_name", "note")
    readonly_fields = ("image_path", "file_sha256", "inspect_image", "reviewed_by", "reviewed_at", "created_at")
    actions = ["approve_selected_images", "reject_selected_images"]

    def inspect_image(self, obj):
        if obj.image_path:
            return format_html(
                '<a href="/admin/accounts/userverificationimage/{}/view/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">View Image</a>',
                obj.id,
            )
        return "No Image"

    def get_urls(self):
        from django.urls import path
        urls = super().get_urls()
        custom_urls = [
            path(
                "<int:image_id>/view/",
                self.admin_site.admin_view(self.secure_view_image),
                name="userverificationimage-secure-view",
            ),
        ]
        return custom_urls + urls

    def secure_view_image(self, request, image_id):
        from django.http import Http404, FileResponse
        from apps.common.permissions import ensure_admin
        ensure_admin(request.user)
        img = UserVerificationImage.objects.filter(id=image_id).first()
        if not img or not img.image_path:
            raise Http404("Image not found")
        return FileResponse(img.image_path.open("rb"), content_type="image/jpeg")

    @admin.action(description="Approve selected verification images")
    def approve_selected_images(self, request, queryset):
        for img in queryset:
            try:
                review_verification_image(
                    actor=request.user,
                    image=img,
                    status="approved",
                    note="Approved via admin action",
                )
            except Exception as e:
                self.message_user(request, f"Error approving image #{img.id}: {e}", level="error")

    @admin.action(description="Reject selected verification images")
    def reject_selected_images(self, request, queryset):
        for img in queryset:
            try:
                review_verification_image(
                    actor=request.user,
                    image=img,
                    status="rejected",
                    note="Rejected via admin action",
                )
            except Exception as e:
                self.message_user(request, f"Error rejecting image #{img.id}: {e}", level="error")


@admin.register(AuthOtp)
class AuthOtpAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("phone_hash", "purpose", "attempt_count", "expires_at", "consumed_at", "created_at")
    list_filter = ("purpose",)
    search_fields = ("phone_hash",)
    readonly_fields = ("phone_hash", "code_hash", "expires_at", "consumed_at", "created_at")

# apps/accounts/authentication.py
from rest_framework.authentication import TokenAuthentication
from apps.common.permissions import ensure_active_account


class BlockedAwareTokenAuthentication(TokenAuthentication):
    def authenticate_credentials(self, key):
        user, token = super().authenticate_credentials(key)
        ensure_active_account(user)
        return (user, token)

# apps/accounts/models.py
from django.contrib.auth.models import AbstractBaseUser, BaseUserManager, PermissionsMixin
from django.contrib.postgres.indexes import GinIndex
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import models
from django.db.models import Q

from apps.common.constants import (
    ACCOUNT_ACTIVE,
    ACCOUNT_BLOCKED,
    ACCOUNT_CLOSED,
    COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
    COORDINATOR_PENDING,
    COORDINATOR_REVOKED,
    COORDINATOR_SUSPENDED,
    IMAGE_DRIVER_LICENSE,
    IMAGE_ID_LICENSE,
    IMAGE_MOCK_SELFIE,
    VERIFICATION_NONE,
    VERIFICATION_PENDING,
    VERIFICATION_REJECTED,
    VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
)
from apps.common.fields import ChoiceArrayField


class UserManager(BaseUserManager):
    def create_user(self, phone_hash, display_name, password=None, **extra_fields):
        if not phone_hash:
            raise ValueError("The phone_hash must be set.")
        if not display_name:
            raise ValueError("The display_name must be set.")

        extra_fields.setdefault("is_staff", False)
        extra_fields.setdefault("is_superuser", False)

        user = self.model(
            phone_hash=phone_hash,
            display_name=display_name,
            **extra_fields,
        )
        if password:
            user.set_password(password)
        else:
            user.set_unusable_password()
        user.save(using=self._db)
        return user

    def create_superuser(self, phone_hash, display_name, password=None, **extra_fields):
        extra_fields.setdefault("is_staff", True)
        extra_fields.setdefault("is_superuser", True)
        extra_fields.setdefault("is_admin", True)

        if not password:
            raise ValueError("Superusers must have a password.")

        return self.create_user(phone_hash, display_name, password, **extra_fields)


class User(AbstractBaseUser, PermissionsMixin):
    ACCOUNT_STATUS_CHOICES = [
        (ACCOUNT_ACTIVE, "Active"),
        (ACCOUNT_BLOCKED, "Blocked"),
        (ACCOUNT_CLOSED, "Closed"),
    ]

    BLOOD_TYPE_CHOICES = [
        ("A+", "A+"),
        ("A-", "A-"),
        ("B+", "B+"),
        ("B-", "B-"),
        ("AB+", "AB+"),
        ("AB-", "AB-"),
        ("O+", "O+"),
        ("O-", "O-"),
        ("unknown", "Unknown"),
    ]

    VERIFICATION_STATUS_CHOICES = [
        (VERIFICATION_NONE, "None"),
        (VERIFICATION_PENDING, "Pending"),
        (VERIFICATION_VERIFIED, "Verified"),
        (VERIFICATION_REJECTED, "Rejected"),
    ]

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)

    # Auth
    phone_hash = models.CharField(max_length=128, unique=True, db_index=True)
    phone_enc = models.TextField(help_text="Encrypted phone representation")
    display_name = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    password = models.CharField(max_length=128, null=True, blank=True)

    is_admin = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_staff = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    account_status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=ACCOUNT_STATUS_CHOICES,
        default=ACCOUNT_ACTIVE,
    )
    blocked_until = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    preferred_language = models.CharField(max_length=10, default="en")
    home_area = models.CharField(max_length=150, null=True, blank=True)

    # Emergency Contact
    emergency_contact_name = models.CharField(max_length=150, null=True, blank=True)
    emergency_contact_phone_enc = models.TextField(null=True, blank=True)

    # Charity / Volunteering attributes
    blood_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=10,
        choices=BLOOD_TYPE_CHOICES,
        default="unknown",
    )
    is_willing_to_donate = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_willing_to_donate_blood = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_willing_to_donate_items = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    # Physical / Capability hints
    physical_ok = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    good_eyesight = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    needs_glasses = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    can_lift_heavy = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    can_work_outdoor = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    has_leader_experience = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    new_to_volunteer = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    # Free-form skills
    skills = ChoiceArrayField(
        models.CharField(max_length=50),
        default=list,
        blank=True,
    )

    # Recruiting preferences
    needs_meal_support = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    needs_travel_support = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    accepts_unpaid = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    # Identity verification
    verification_status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=VERIFICATION_STATUS_CHOICES,
        default=VERIFICATION_NONE,
    )

    last_login = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    objects = UserManager()

    USERNAME_FIELD = "phone_hash"
    REQUIRED_FIELDS = ["display_name"]

    class Meta:
        db_table = "users"
        verbose_name = "User"
        verbose_name_plural = "Users"
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(blocked_until__isnull=True) | Q(account_status="blocked"),
                name="ck_users_blocked_until",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(is_willing_to_donate=True)
                | (
                    Q(is_willing_to_donate_blood=False)
                    & Q(is_willing_to_donate_items=False)
                ),
                name="ck_users_donation_consistency",
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["account_status"], name="idx_users_account_status"),
            models.Index(fields=["verification_status"], name="idx_users_verif_status"),
            models.Index(
                fields=["is_admin"],
                name="idx_users_is_admin",
                condition=Q(is_admin=True),
            ),
            models.Index(
                fields=["blood_type"],
                name="idx_users_blood_donors",
                condition=Q(is_willing_to_donate_blood=True),
            ),
            GinIndex(fields=["skills"], name="idx_users_skills_gin"),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.display_name} (ID: {self.id})"

    def clean(self):
        super().clean()
        if self.blocked_until is not None and self.account_status != ACCOUNT_BLOCKED:
            raise ValidationError(
                {"blocked_until": "blocked_until may only be set when account_status is 'blocked'."}
            )
        if (self.is_willing_to_donate_blood or self.is_willing_to_donate_items) and not self.is_willing_to_donate:
            raise ValidationError(
                {"is_willing_to_donate": "is_willing_to_donate must be True if willing to donate blood or items."}
            )


class CoordinatorProfile(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        (COORDINATOR_PENDING, "Pending"),
        (COORDINATOR_APPROVED, "Approved"),
        (COORDINATOR_SUSPENDED, "Suspended"),
        (COORDINATOR_REVOKED, "Revoked"),
    ]

    user = models.OneToOneField(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        primary_key=True,
        related_name="coordinator_profile",
    )
    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=STATUS_CHOICES,
        default=COORDINATOR_PENDING,
    )
    approved_by = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="approved_coordinators",
    )
    approved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    notes = models.TextField(null=True, blank=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "coordinator_profiles"
        verbose_name = "Coordinator Profile"
        verbose_name_plural = "Coordinator Profiles"
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(status="pending") | Q(approved_at__isnull=False),
                name="ck_coordinator_approval",
            ),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Coordinator: {self.user.display_name} [{self.status}]"

    def clean(self):
        super().clean()
        if self.status != COORDINATOR_PENDING and self.approved_at is None:
            raise ValidationError(
                {"approved_at": "approved_at must be set when status is not 'pending'."}
            )


class UserCharityInterest(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    user = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="charity_interests",
    )
    field_code = models.ForeignKey(
        "events.CharityField",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        db_column="field_code",
        related_name="user_interests",
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "user_charity_interests"
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["user", "field_code"],
                name="uq_user_charity_interest",
            ),
        ]
        verbose_name = "User Charity Interest"
        verbose_name_plural = "User Charity Interests"

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.display_name} -> {self.field_code_id}"


class UserVerificationImage(models.Model):
    IMAGE_TYPE_CHOICES = [
        (IMAGE_MOCK_SELFIE, "Mock Selfie"),
        (IMAGE_ID_LICENSE, "ID License"),
        (IMAGE_DRIVER_LICENSE, "Driver License"),
    ]

    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        (VERIFICATION_PENDING, "Pending"),
        ("approved", "Approved"),
        (VERIFICATION_VERIFIED, "Verified"),
        (VERIFICATION_REJECTED, "Rejected"),
    ]

    COMPARISON_STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ("pending", "Pending"),
        ("match", "Match"),
        ("no_match", "No Match"),
        ("needs_review", "Needs Review"),
    ]

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    user = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="verification_images",
    )
    image_type = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=IMAGE_TYPE_CHOICES)
    image_path = models.ImageField(upload_to="verification_images/")
    file_format = models.CharField(max_length=10, default="jpeg")
    file_sha256 = models.CharField(max_length=64, null=True, blank=True)

    comparison_score = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=5,
        decimal_places=4,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
    )
    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=STATUS_CHOICES,
        default=VERIFICATION_PENDING,
    )
    comparison_status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=COMPARISON_STATUS_CHOICES,
        default="pending",
    )
    is_current = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    reviewed_by = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="reviewed_verification_images",
    )
    reviewed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    note = models.TextField(null=True, blank=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "user_verification_images"
        verbose_name = "User Verification Image"
        verbose_name_plural = "User Verification Images"
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["user", "image_type"],
                condition=Q(is_current=True),
                name="uq_current_verification_image",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(status="pending")
                | (Q(reviewed_by__isnull=False) & Q(reviewed_at__isnull=False)),
                name="ck_verification_review",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(comparison_score__isnull=True)
                | (Q(comparison_score__gte=0) & Q(comparison_score__lte=1)),
                name="ck_verification_score_range",
            ),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.display_name} - {self.image_type} ({self.status})"

    def clean(self):
        super().clean()
        if self.status != VERIFICATION_PENDING:
            if self.reviewed_by is None or self.reviewed_at is None:
                raise ValidationError(
                    "Reviewed images must have reviewed_by and reviewed_at set."
                )
        if self.comparison_score is not None:
            if not (0 <= self.comparison_score <= 1):
                raise ValidationError(
                    {"comparison_score": "comparison_score must be between 0 and 1."}
                )


class AuthOtp(models.Model):
    PURPOSE_CHOICES = [
        ("login", "Login"),
        ("qr_registration", "QR Registration"),
    ]

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    phone_hash = models.CharField(max_length=128, db_index=True)
    code_hash = models.CharField(max_length=128)
    purpose = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=PURPOSE_CHOICES, default="login")
    attempt_count = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    expires_at = models.DateTimeField()
    consumed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "auth_otp"
        verbose_name = "Auth OTP"
        verbose_name_plural = "Auth OTPs"
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(attempt_count__gte=0),
                name="ck_auth_otp_attempt_count",
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["phone_hash", "-created_at"], name="idx_auth_otp_phone_created"),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"OTP {self.purpose} for {self.phone_hash[:8]}..."

    def clean(self):
        super().clean()
        if self.attempt_count < 0:
            raise ValidationError({"attempt_count": "attempt_count cannot be negative."})

class VerificationImageUploadSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    image_type = serializers.ChoiceField(
        choices=[
            (IMAGE_MOCK_SELFIE, "Mock Selfie"),
            (IMAGE_ID_LICENSE, "ID License"),
            (IMAGE_DRIVER_LICENSE, "Driver License"),
        ]
    )
    image = serializers.ImageField()


class CharityInterestsUpdateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    codes = serializers.ListField(
        child=serializers.CharField(max_length=50),
        allow_empty=True,
    )


class CoordinatorProfileSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = CoordinatorProfile
        fields = [
            "status",
            "approved_at",
            "notes",
        ]
        read_only_fields = ["status", "approved_at"]


class CoordinatorApplySerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    notes = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, default="")
