# apps/events/services.py
from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.audit.services import log_action
from apps.common.constants import (
    ACTIVE_APPLICATION_STATUSES,
    ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
    APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
    APPLICATION_PENDING,
    APPLICATION_REJECTED,
    APPLICATION_WAITLISTED,
    APPLICATION_WITHDRAWN,
    ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED,
    ASSIGNMENT_COMPLETED,
    ATTENDANCE_ABSENT,
    ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN,
    ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED,
    ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
    EVENT_ACTIVE,
    EVENT_CANCELLED,
    EVENT_COMPLETED,
    EVENT_DRAFT,
    EVENT_PUBLISHED,
)
from apps.common.exceptions import (
    CapacityFullError,
    DomainError,
    InvalidStateError,
    PermissionDeniedError,
)
from apps.common.permissions import (
    ensure_active_account,
    ensure_admin,
    ensure_approved_coordinator,
    ensure_event_manager,
    is_admin,
)
from apps.events.models import CharityField, Event, EventApplication, EventCharityField, EventPost


def create_event(actor, data: dict, charity_codes: list[str] = ()) -> Event:
    """
    Coordinator or Admin creates a new event in draft status.
    """
    if not is_admin(actor):
        ensure_approved_coordinator(actor)

    clean_data = dict(data)
    # Ensure status starts at draft and coordinator is actor
    clean_data["status"] = EVENT_DRAFT
    clean_data["coordinator"] = actor
    clean_data.setdefault("onboarding_version", 1)

    # Pop charity codes if included in data dict
    codes = charity_codes or clean_data.pop("charity_codes", ())

    with transaction.atomic():
        event = Event.objects.create(**clean_data)
        if codes:
            set_event_charity_fields(actor, event, codes)

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="create_event",
            entity_type="event",
            entity_id=event.id,
            after_data={"title": event.title, "start_at": str(event.start_at)},
        )

    return event


def update_event(actor, event: Event, data: dict) -> Event:
    """
    Coordinator or Admin updates event fields.
    If onboarding_text changes, onboarding_version is incremented automatically.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    clean_data = dict(data)
    # Do not allow arbitrary status jumps via update_event
    clean_data.pop("status", None)
    clean_data.pop("coordinator", None)

    with transaction.atomic():
        # Check if onboarding_text changed
        if "onboarding_text" in clean_data:
            new_text = clean_data["onboarding_text"]
            if new_text != event.onboarding_text:
                event.onboarding_version += 1

        codes = clean_data.pop("charity_codes", None)
        if codes is not None:
            set_event_charity_fields(actor, event, codes)

        for field, value in clean_data.items():
            if hasattr(event, field):
                setattr(event, field, value)

        event.clean()
        event.save()

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="update_event",
            entity_type="event",
            entity_id=event.id,
            after_data={k: str(v) for k, v in clean_data.items()},
        )

    return event


def publish_event(actor, event: Event) -> Event:
    """
    Transition event from draft to published.
    Requires at least one post.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    if event.status != EVENT_DRAFT:
        raise InvalidStateError(f"Cannot publish event in status '{event.status}'. Must be 'draft'.")

    if not event.posts.exists():
        raise DomainError("Event must have at least one post before publishing.", code="post_required")

    with transaction.atomic():
        event.status = EVENT_PUBLISHED
        event.save(update_fields=["status"])

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="publish_event",
            entity_type="event",
            entity_id=event.id,
        )

    return event


def activate_event(actor, event: Event) -> Event:
    """
    Transition event from published to active.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    if event.status != EVENT_PUBLISHED:
        raise InvalidStateError(f"Cannot activate event in status '{event.status}'. Must be 'published'.")

    with transaction.atomic():
        event.status = EVENT_ACTIVE
        event.save(update_fields=["status"])

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="activate_event",
            entity_type="event",
            entity_id=event.id,
        )

    return event


def complete_event(actor, event: Event) -> Event:
    """
    Transition event from active to completed.
    Cascades status:
    - Active/confirmed assignments become completed
    - Checked-in attendances become completed
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    if event.status != EVENT_ACTIVE:
        raise InvalidStateError(f"Cannot complete event in status '{event.status}'. Must be 'active'.")

    from apps.ops.models import Assignment, Attendance

    with transaction.atomic():
        event.status = EVENT_COMPLETED
        event.save(update_fields=["status"])

        # Complete active assignments
        Assignment.objects.filter(
            event=event,
            status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
        ).update(status=ASSIGNMENT_COMPLETED)

        # Complete checked-in attendances
        Attendance.objects.filter(
            event=event,
            status=ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN,
        ).update(status=ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED)

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="complete_event",
            entity_type="event",
            entity_id=event.id,
        )

    return event


def cancel_event(actor, event: Event, reason: str = "") -> Event:
    """
    Cancel an event (from published or active).
    Cascades:
    - Active assignments become cancelled
    - Expected attendances (not checked in) become absent
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    if event.status not in (EVENT_PUBLISHED, EVENT_ACTIVE):
        raise InvalidStateError(
            f"Cannot cancel event in status '{event.status}'. Must be 'published' or 'active'."
        )

    from apps.ops.models import Assignment, Attendance

    with transaction.atomic():
        event.status = EVENT_CANCELLED
        event.save(update_fields=["status"])

        # Cancel active assignments
        Assignment.objects.filter(
            event=event,
            status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
        ).update(status=ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED)

        # Mark expected attendance as absent
        Attendance.objects.filter(
            event=event,
            status=ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
        ).update(status=ATTENDANCE_ABSENT)

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="cancel_event",
            entity_type="event",
            entity_id=event.id,
            after_data={"reason": reason},
        )

    return event


def set_event_charity_fields(actor, event: Event, charity_codes: list[str]) -> None:
    """
    Replace event charity fields atomically.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    with transaction.atomic():
        valid_fields = list(CharityField.objects.filter(code__in=charity_codes, active=True))
        EventCharityField.objects.filter(event=event).delete()
        EventCharityField.objects.bulk_create(
            [EventCharityField(event=event, field_code=field) for field in valid_fields]
        )


def add_post(actor, event: Event, data: dict) -> EventPost:
    """
    Add recruiting post to event.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    with transaction.atomic():
        clean_data = dict(data)
        clean_data["event"] = event
        clean_data.setdefault("status", "open")
        post = EventPost.objects.create(**clean_data)

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="add_post",
            entity_type="event_post",
            entity_id=post.id,
            after_data={"title": post.title, "required_headcount": post.required_headcount},
        )

    return post


def update_post(actor, post: EventPost, data: dict) -> EventPost:
    """
    Update post details.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, post.event)

    clean_data = dict(data)
    clean_data.pop("event", None)

    with transaction.atomic():
        for field, value in clean_data.items():
            if hasattr(post, field):
                setattr(post, field, value)

        post.clean()
        post.save()

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="update_post",
            entity_type="event_post",
            entity_id=post.id,
            after_data={k: str(v) for k, v in clean_data.items()},
        )

    return post


def close_post(actor, post: EventPost) -> EventPost:
    """
    Close post to new applications.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, post.event)

    with transaction.atomic():
        post.status = "closed"
        post.save(update_fields=["status"])

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="close_post",
            entity_type="event_post",
            entity_id=post.id,
        )

    return post


def apply_to_event(
    user,
    event: Event,
    post: EventPost = None,
    source: str = "browse",
    qr_token=None,
) -> EventApplication:
    """
    Volunteer applies to an event (or specific post in an event).
    Enforces active account, event status, deadlines, duplicates, and capacity gating.
    Lock order: Event -> Post -> Application.
    """
    ensure_active_account(user)

    if event.deleted_at is not None or event.status not in (EVENT_PUBLISHED, EVENT_ACTIVE):
        raise InvalidStateError(
            f"Event is not open for applications. Current status: '{event.status}'."
        )

    if event.application_deadline and event.application_deadline <= timezone.now():
        raise DomainError("Application deadline has passed.", code="deadline_passed")

    # Check existing active applications for (event, user)
    if EventApplication.objects.filter(
        event=event,
        user=user,
        status__in=ACTIVE_APPLICATION_STATUSES,
    ).exists():
        raise DomainError(
            "User already has an active application for this event.",
            code="duplicate_application",
        )

    if source == "qr_walkin":
        if not qr_token:
            raise DomainError("QR token required for walk-in registration.", code="qr_token_required")
        if qr_token.expires_at <= timezone.now() or qr_token.revoked_at is not None:
            raise DomainError("QR token is expired or revoked.", code="invalid_qr_token")

    conditions_snapshot = {
        "meals_provided": event.meals_provided,
        "lodging_provided": event.lodging_provided,
        "travel_support_provided": event.travel_support_provided,
        "compensation_kind": event.compensation_kind,
        "compensation_note": event.compensation_note,
        "onboarding_version": event.onboarding_version,
    }

    with transaction.atomic():
        # Lock event
        locked_event = Event.objects.select_for_update().get(id=event.id)
        locked_post = None
        if post:
            locked_post = EventPost.objects.select_for_update().get(id=post.id)

        from apps.ops.models import Assignment

        # Capacity check and status determination
        if locked_event.acceptance_mode == "auto":
            if locked_post:
                active_assignments_count = Assignment.objects.filter(
                    post=locked_post,
                    status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
                ).count()
                capacity = locked_post.required_headcount - active_assignments_count
            else:
                if locked_event.max_participants:
                    accepted_count = EventApplication.objects.filter(
                        event=locked_event,
                        status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
                    ).count()
                    capacity = locked_event.max_participants - accepted_count
                else:
                    capacity = 999999

            if capacity > 0:
                app_status = APPLICATION_ACCEPTED
                decided_at = timezone.now()
            else:
                app_status = APPLICATION_WAITLISTED
                decided_at = timezone.now()
        else:
            app_status = APPLICATION_PENDING
            decided_at = None

        application = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=locked_event,
            post=locked_post,
            user=user,
            source=source,
            qr_token=qr_token,
            status=app_status,
            decided_at=decided_at,
            conditions_snapshot=conditions_snapshot,
        )

        if app_status == APPLICATION_ACCEPTED and locked_event.auto_assign_enabled:
            from apps.ops.services import create_assignment_from_application
            create_assignment_from_application(application)

        log_action(
            actor=user,
            action="apply_event",
            entity_type="event_application",
            entity_id=application.id,
            after_data={"status": app_status, "source": source},
        )

    return application


def accept_application(
    actor,
    application: EventApplication,
    post: EventPost = None,
):
    """
    Coordinator or Admin accepts an application.
    Verifies capacity on target post or event.
    Returns (application, assignment).
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, application.event)

    if application.status == APPLICATION_ACCEPTED:
        raise InvalidStateError("Application is already accepted.")

    if application.status not in (APPLICATION_PENDING, APPLICATION_WAITLISTED):
        raise InvalidStateError(
            f"Cannot accept application in status '{application.status}'."
        )

    target_post = post or application.post

    with transaction.atomic():
        locked_app = EventApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application.id)
        from apps.ops.models import Assignment

        if target_post:
            locked_post = EventPost.objects.select_for_update().get(id=target_post.id)
            active_post_assigns = Assignment.objects.filter(
                post=locked_post,
                status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
            ).count()
            if active_post_assigns >= locked_post.required_headcount:
                raise CapacityFullError("Target post capacity is full.")
            locked_app.post = locked_post
        elif locked_app.event.max_participants:
            accepted_apps_count = EventApplication.objects.filter(
                event=locked_app.event,
                status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
            ).exclude(id=locked_app.id).count()
            if accepted_apps_count >= locked_app.event.max_participants:
                raise CapacityFullError("Event capacity is full.")

        locked_app.status = APPLICATION_ACCEPTED
        locked_app.decided_by = actor
        locked_app.decided_at = timezone.now()
        locked_app.save()

        from apps.ops.services import create_assignment_from_application
        assignment = create_assignment_from_application(locked_app, actor=actor)

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="accept_application",
            entity_type="event_application",
            entity_id=locked_app.id,
        )

    return locked_app, assignment


def reject_application(
    actor,
    application: EventApplication,
    reason: str = "",
) -> EventApplication:
    """
    Coordinator or Admin rejects an application.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, application.event)

    if application.status == APPLICATION_REJECTED:
        return application

    with transaction.atomic():
        application.status = APPLICATION_REJECTED
        application.decided_by = actor
        application.decided_at = timezone.now()
        if reason:
            application.note = reason
        application.save()

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="reject_application",
            entity_type="event_application",
            entity_id=application.id,
            after_data={"reason": reason},
        )

    return application


def waitlist_application(
    actor,
    application: EventApplication,
) -> EventApplication:
    """
    Coordinator or Admin waitlists an application.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, application.event)

    with transaction.atomic():
        application.status = APPLICATION_WAITLISTED
        application.decided_by = actor
        application.decided_at = timezone.now()
        application.save()

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="waitlist_application",
            entity_type="event_application",
            entity_id=application.id,
        )

    return application


def withdraw_application(
    user,
    application: EventApplication,
) -> EventApplication:
    """
    Volunteer withdraws own application.
    Cancels any active assignment.
    """
    if application.user_id != user.id:
        raise PermissionDeniedError("Cannot withdraw another volunteer's application.")

    if application.status not in (APPLICATION_PENDING, APPLICATION_ACCEPTED, APPLICATION_WAITLISTED):
        raise InvalidStateError(
            f"Cannot withdraw application in status '{application.status}'."
        )

    from apps.ops.models import Assignment

    with transaction.atomic():
        Assignment.objects.filter(
            application=application,
            status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
        ).update(status=ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED)

        application.status = APPLICATION_WITHDRAWN
        application.save()

        log_action(
            actor=user,
            action="withdraw_application",
            entity_type="event_application",
            entity_id=application.id,
        )

    return application

# apps/events/urls.py
from django.urls import path

from apps.events.views import (
    ApplicationAcceptView,
    ApplicationRejectView,
    ApplicationWaitlistView,
    ApplicationWithdrawView,
    CharityFieldListView,
    CoordinatorEventDetailPatchView,
    CoordinatorEventListCreateView,
    EventActivateView,
    EventApplicationsListView,
    EventApplyView,
    EventCancelView,
    EventCompleteView,
    EventPostListCreateView,
    EventPublishView,
    EventRosterView,
    EventUnassignedView,
    PostDetailPatchView,
    PublicEventDetailView,
    PublicEventListView,
)

urlpatterns = [
    # Charity Fields
    path("charity-fields/", CharityFieldListView.as_view(), name="charity-fields-list"),

    # Public Events
    path("events/", PublicEventListView.as_view(), name="public-events-list"),
    path("events/<int:pk>/", PublicEventDetailView.as_view(), name="public-event-detail"),

    # Coordinator Event Management
    path("coordinator/events/", CoordinatorEventListCreateView.as_view(), name="coordinator-events-list-create"),
    path("coordinator/events/<int:pk>/", CoordinatorEventDetailPatchView.as_view(), name="coordinator-event-patch"),

    # Event Lifecycle Actions
    path("events/<int:pk>/publish/", EventPublishView.as_view(), name="event-publish"),
    path("events/<int:pk>/activate/", EventActivateView.as_view(), name="event-activate"),
    path("events/<int:pk>/complete/", EventCompleteView.as_view(), name="event-complete"),
    path("events/<int:pk>/cancel/", EventCancelView.as_view(), name="event-cancel"),

    # Event Posts
    path("events/<int:pk>/posts/", EventPostListCreateView.as_view(), name="event-posts-list-create"),
    path("posts/<int:pk>/", PostDetailPatchView.as_view(), name="post-patch"),

    # Applications
    path("events/<int:pk>/apply/", EventApplyView.as_view(), name="event-apply"),
    path("events/<int:pk>/applications/", EventApplicationsListView.as_view(), name="event-applications-list"),
    path("events/<int:pk>/roster/", EventRosterView.as_view(), name="event-roster"),
    path("events/<int:pk>/unassigned/", EventUnassignedView.as_view(), name="event-unassigned"),

    # Application Decisions
    path("applications/<int:pk>/accept/", ApplicationAcceptView.as_view(), name="application-accept"),
    path("applications/<int:pk>/reject/", ApplicationRejectView.as_view(), name="application-reject"),
    path("applications/<int:pk>/waitlist/", ApplicationWaitlistView.as_view(), name="application-waitlist"),
    path("applications/<int:pk>/withdraw/", ApplicationWithdrawView.as_view(), name="application-withdraw"),
]

# apps/events/views.py
from django.db.models import Q
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.permissions import AllowAny, IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.common.constants import (
    ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
    EVENT_ACTIVE,
    EVENT_PUBLISHED,
)
from apps.common.exceptions import PermissionDeniedError
from apps.common.permissions import (
    IsActiveAccount,
    ensure_event_manager,
    is_admin,
    is_event_manager,
)
from apps.events.models import CharityField, Event, EventApplication, EventPost
from apps.events.serializers import (
    CharityFieldSerializer,
    EventApplicationCreateSerializer,
    EventApplicationSerializer,
    EventCreateUpdateSerializer,
    EventPostCreateSerializer,
    EventPostSerializer,
    EventSerializer,
)
from apps.events.services import (
    accept_application,
    activate_event,
    add_post,
    apply_to_event,
    cancel_event,
    close_post,
    complete_event,
    create_event,
    publish_event,
    reject_application,
    update_event,
    update_post,
    waitlist_application,
    withdraw_application,
)
from apps.ops.models import Assignment, Attendance, QrToken


class CharityFieldListView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]

    def get(self, request):
        fields = CharityField.objects.filter(active=True).order_by("sort_order")
        return Response(CharityFieldSerializer(fields, many=True).data)


class PublicEventListView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]

    def get(self, request):
        qs = Event.objects.visible().filter(status__in=[EVENT_PUBLISHED, EVENT_ACTIVE])

        charity_field = request.query_params.get("charity_field")
        if charity_field:
            qs = qs.filter(charity_links__field_code_id=charity_field)

        date_from = request.query_params.get("date_from")
        if date_from:
            qs = qs.filter(start_at__gte=date_from)

        date_to = request.query_params.get("date_to")
        if date_to:
            qs = qs.filter(start_at__lte=date_to)

        q = request.query_params.get("q")
        if q:
            qs = qs.filter(
                Q(title__icontains=q)
                | Q(description__icontains=q)
                | Q(location_name__icontains=q)
                | Q(landmark_text__icontains=q)
            )

        qs = qs.distinct().order_by("start_at")
        return Response(EventSerializer(qs, many=True).data)


class PublicEventDetailView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]

    def get(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event.objects.visible(), pk=pk)
        if event.status not in (EVENT_PUBLISHED, EVENT_ACTIVE):
            if not is_event_manager(request.user, event):
                raise PermissionDeniedError("Event is not publicly visible.")
        return Response(EventSerializer(event).data)


class CoordinatorEventListCreateView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def get(self, request):
        if is_admin(request.user):
            qs = Event.objects.visible()
        else:
            qs = Event.objects.visible().filter(coordinator=request.user)
        qs = qs.order_by("-created_at")
        return Response(EventSerializer(qs, many=True).data)

    def post(self, request):
        serializer = EventCreateUpdateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        event = create_event(
            actor=request.user,
            data=serializer.validated_data,
        )
        return Response(EventSerializer(event).data, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class CoordinatorEventDetailPatchView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def patch(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event.objects.visible(), pk=pk)
        serializer = EventCreateUpdateSerializer(data=request.data, partial=True)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        updated_event = update_event(
            actor=request.user,
            event=event,
            data=serializer.validated_data,
        )
        return Response(EventSerializer(updated_event).data)


class EventPublishView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event.objects.visible(), pk=pk)
        published = publish_event(request.user, event)
        return Response(EventSerializer(published).data)


class EventActivateView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event.objects.visible(), pk=pk)
        activated = activate_event(request.user, event)
        return Response(EventSerializer(activated).data)


class EventCompleteView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event.objects.visible(), pk=pk)
        completed = complete_event(request.user, event)
        return Response(EventSerializer(completed).data)


class EventCancelView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event.objects.visible(), pk=pk)
        reason = request.data.get("reason", "")
        cancelled = cancel_event(request.user, event, reason=reason)
        return Response(EventSerializer(cancelled).data)


class EventPostListCreateView(APIView):
    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.request.method == "GET":
            return [AllowAny()]
        return [IsAuthenticated(), IsActiveAccount()]

    def get(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event.objects.visible(), pk=pk)
        posts = event.posts.all().order_by("priority", "id")
        return Response(EventPostSerializer(posts, many=True).data)

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event.objects.visible(), pk=pk)
        serializer = EventPostCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        post = add_post(request.user, event, serializer.validated_data)
        return Response(EventPostSerializer(post).data, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class PostDetailPatchView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def patch(self, request, pk):
        post = get_object_or_404(EventPost, pk=pk)
        if request.data.get("status") == "closed":
            closed_post = close_post(request.user, post)
            return Response(EventPostSerializer(closed_post).data)
        updated_post = update_post(request.user, post, request.data)
        return Response(EventPostSerializer(updated_post).data)


class EventApplyView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event.objects.visible(), pk=pk)
        serializer = EventApplicationCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        post = None
        post_id = serializer.validated_data.get("post_id")
        if post_id:
            post = get_object_or_404(event.posts, pk=post_id)

        qr_token = None
        qr_token_id = serializer.validated_data.get("qr_token_id")
        if qr_token_id:
            qr_token = get_object_or_404(QrToken, pk=qr_token_id)

        source = serializer.validated_data.get("source", "browse")
        application = apply_to_event(
            user=request.user,
            event=event,
            post=post,
            source=source,
            qr_token=qr_token,
        )
        return Response(
            EventApplicationSerializer(application).data,
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )


class EventApplicationsListView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def get(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event.objects.visible(), pk=pk)
        ensure_event_manager(request.user, event)
        apps = event.applications.all().order_by("-created_at")
        return Response(EventApplicationSerializer(apps, many=True).data)


class EventRosterView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def get(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event.objects.visible(), pk=pk)
        ensure_event_manager(request.user, event)

        assignments = (
            Assignment.objects.filter(event=event)
            .select_related("user", "post")
            .order_by("group_code", "id")
        )
        attendances = {
            att.user_id: att.status
            for att in Attendance.objects.filter(event=event)
        }

        roster = []
        for a in assignments:
            roster.append(
                {
                    "assignment_id": a.id,
                    "user_id": a.user_id,
                    "display_name": a.user.display_name,
                    "post_id": a.post_id,
                    "post_title": a.post.title if a.post else None,
                    "group_code": a.group_code,
                    "is_group_leader": a.is_group_leader,
                    "status": a.status,
                    "attendance_status": attendances.get(a.user_id, "none"),
                }
            )

        return Response({"roster": roster})


class EventUnassignedView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def get(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event.objects.visible(), pk=pk)
        ensure_event_manager(request.user, event)

        unassigned_apps = (
            EventApplication.objects.filter(event=event, status="accepted")
            .filter(~Q(assignments__status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES))
            .distinct()
        )
        return Response(EventApplicationSerializer(unassigned_apps, many=True).data)


class ApplicationAcceptView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        app = get_object_or_404(EventApplication, pk=pk)
        post = None
        post_id = request.data.get("post_id")
        if post_id:
            post = get_object_or_404(app.event.posts, pk=post_id)

        accepted_app, assignment = accept_application(
            actor=request.user,
            application=app,
            post=post,
        )
        return Response(
            {
                "application": EventApplicationSerializer(accepted_app).data,
                "assigned": bool(assignment),
            }
        )


class ApplicationRejectView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        app = get_object_or_404(EventApplication, pk=pk)
        reason = request.data.get("reason", "")
        rejected_app = reject_application(
            actor=request.user,
            application=app,
            reason=reason,
        )
        return Response(EventApplicationSerializer(rejected_app).data)


class ApplicationWaitlistView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        app = get_object_or_404(EventApplication, pk=pk)
        waitlisted_app = waitlist_application(
            actor=request.user,
            application=app,
        )
        return Response(EventApplicationSerializer(waitlisted_app).data)


class ApplicationWithdrawView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        app = get_object_or_404(EventApplication, pk=pk)
        withdrawn_app = withdraw_application(
            user=request.user,
            application=app,
        )
        return Response(EventApplicationSerializer(withdrawn_app).data)

# apps/ops/admin.py
from django.contrib import admin

from apps.audit.services import hide_feedback
from apps.ops.models import (
    Assignment,
    Attendance,
    Feedback,
    OnboardingAcknowledgment,
    QrToken,
)


@admin.register(QrToken)
class QrTokenAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = (
        "id",
        "event",
        "purpose",
        "used_count",
        "max_uses",
        "expires_at",
        "revoked_at",
        "created_at",
    )
    list_filter = ("purpose",)
    search_fields = ("event__title", "token_hash")
    readonly_fields = ("token_hash", "used_count", "created_at")


@admin.register(Assignment)
class AssignmentAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = (
        "id",
        "event",
        "user",
        "post",
        "status",
        "group_code",
        "is_group_leader",
        "assigned_by",
        "created_at",
    )
    list_filter = ("status", "assigned_by", "is_group_leader")
    search_fields = ("user__display_name", "event__title", "group_code")
    readonly_fields = ("created_at", "conditions_snapshot")


@admin.register(Attendance)
class AttendanceAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = (
        "id",
        "event",
        "user",
        "status",
        "checkin_at",
        "checkout_at",
        "method",
        "recorded_by",
    )
    list_filter = ("status", "method")
    search_fields = ("user__display_name", "event__title")
    readonly_fields = ("checkin_at", "checkout_at", "created_at")


@admin.register(OnboardingAcknowledgment)
class OnboardingAcknowledgmentAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = (
        "id",
        "event",
        "user",
        "onboarding_version",
        "method",
        "acknowledged_at",
    )
    list_filter = ("onboarding_version", "method")
    search_fields = ("user__display_name", "event__title")
    readonly_fields = ("acknowledged_at",)


@admin.register(Feedback)
class FeedbackAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = (
        "id",
        "event",
        "rater_user",
        "target_user",
        "direction",
        "score",
        "status",
        "created_at",
    )
    list_filter = ("direction", "status", "score")
    search_fields = ("rater_user__display_name", "target_user__display_name", "event__title")
    actions = ["hide_selected_feedbacks"]

    @admin.action(description="Hide selected feedbacks")
    def hide_selected_feedbacks(self, request, queryset):
        for fb in queryset:
            if fb.status != "hidden":
                try:
                    hide_feedback(admin=request.user, feedback=fb, reason="Hidden via admin action")
                except Exception as e:
                    self.message_user(request, f"Error hiding feedback #{fb.id}: {e}", level="error")

# apps/ops/models.py
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import models
from django.db.models import F, Q

from apps.common.constants import (
    ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,
    ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED,
    ASSIGNMENT_COMPLETED,
    ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED,
    ATTENDANCE_ABSENT,
    ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN,
    ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED,
    ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
    FEEDBACK_COORDINATOR_TO_VOLUNTEER,
    FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_COORDINATOR,
    FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT,
    QR_CHECKIN,
    QR_JOIN_EVENT,
    QR_WALKIN_REGISTRATION,
)
from apps.common.fields import ChoiceArrayField


class QrToken(models.Model):
    PURPOSE_CHOICES = [
        (QR_WALKIN_REGISTRATION, "Walk-in Registration"),
        (QR_CHECKIN, "Check-in"),
        (QR_JOIN_EVENT, "Join Event"),
    ]

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    event = models.ForeignKey(
        "events.Event",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="qr_tokens",
    )
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="created_qr_tokens",
    )
    purpose = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=PURPOSE_CHOICES)
    token_hash = models.CharField(max_length=128, unique=True)

    max_uses = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
    used_count = models.IntegerField(default=0)

    expires_at = models.DateTimeField()
    revoked_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "qr_tokens"
        verbose_name = "QR Token"
        verbose_name_plural = "QR Tokens"
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(max_uses__isnull=True) | Q(max_uses__gt=0),
                name="ck_qr_max_uses",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(used_count__gte=0),
                name="ck_qr_used_count_nonnegative",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(max_uses__isnull=True) | Q(used_count__lte=F("max_uses")),
                name="ck_qr_used_count",
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["event", "purpose"], name="idx_qr_event_purpose"),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"QR Token ({self.purpose}) for {self.event.title}"

    def clean(self):
        super().clean()
        if self.max_uses is not None and self.max_uses <= 0:
            raise ValidationError({"max_uses": "max_uses must be greater than 0."})
        if self.used_count < 0:
            raise ValidationError({"used_count": "used_count cannot be negative."})
        if self.max_uses is not None and self.used_count > self.max_uses:
            raise ValidationError({"used_count": "used_count cannot exceed max_uses."})


class Assignment(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        (ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED, "Assigned"),
        (ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED, "Confirmed"),
        (ASSIGNMENT_COMPLETED, "Completed"),
        (ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED, "Cancelled"),
    ]

    ASSIGNED_BY_CHOICES = [
        ("auto", "Auto"),
        ("coordinator", "Coordinator"),
        ("admin", "Admin"),
    ]

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    event = models.ForeignKey(
        "events.Event",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="assignments",
    )
    application = models.ForeignKey(
        "events.EventApplication",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="assignments",
    )
    post = models.ForeignKey(
        "events.EventPost",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="assignments",
    )
    user = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="assignments",
    )

    group_code = models.CharField(max_length=50, null=True, blank=True)
    is_group_leader = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=STATUS_CHOICES,
        default=ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,
    )
    assigned_by = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=ASSIGNED_BY_CHOICES,
        default="auto",
    )
    assigned_by_user = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="made_assignments",
    )

    schedule_start = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    schedule_end = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    conditions_snapshot = models.JSONField(null=True, blank=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "assignments"
        verbose_name = "Assignment"
        verbose_name_plural = "Assignments"
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["event", "user"],
                condition=~Q(status=ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED),
                name="uq_assignment_active",
            ),
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["application"],
                condition=~Q(status=ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED),
                name="uq_assignment_application",
            ),
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["event", "group_code"],
                condition=Q(is_group_leader=True) & ~Q(status=ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED),
                name="uq_assignment_group_leader",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=(
                    Q(assigned_by="auto") & Q(assigned_by_user__isnull=True)
                )
                | (
                    ~Q(assigned_by="auto") & Q(assigned_by_user__isnull=False)
                ),
                name="ck_assignment_actor",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(schedule_start__isnull=True)
                | Q(schedule_end__isnull=True)
                | Q(schedule_end__gt=F("schedule_start")),
                name="ck_assignment_schedule",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(is_group_leader=False) | Q(group_code__isnull=False),
                name="ck_assignment_leader_group",
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["event", "user"], name="idx_assign_event_user"),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.display_name} -> {self.event.title} [{self.status}]"

    def clean(self):
        super().clean()
        if self.assigned_by == "auto" and self.assigned_by_user is not None:
            raise ValidationError("assigned_by_user must be null when assigned_by is 'auto'.")
        if self.assigned_by != "auto" and self.assigned_by_user is None:
            raise ValidationError("assigned_by_user must be set when assigned_by is not 'auto'.")
        if self.schedule_start and self.schedule_end and self.schedule_end <= self.schedule_start:
            raise ValidationError({"schedule_end": "schedule_end must be greater than schedule_start."})
        if self.is_group_leader and not self.group_code:
            raise ValidationError({"group_code": "group_code is required when is_group_leader is True."})


class Attendance(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        (ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED, "Expected"),
        (ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN, "Checked In"),
        (ATTENDANCE_ABSENT, "Absent"),
        (ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED, "Completed"),
    ]

    METHOD_CHOICES = [
        ("qr_scan", "QR Scan"),
        ("coordinator_manual", "Coordinator Manual"),
        ("system", "System"),
    ]

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    event = models.ForeignKey(
        "events.Event",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="attendances",
    )
    user = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="attendances",
    )
    assignment = models.ForeignKey(
        Assignment,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="attendances",
    )
    qr_token = models.ForeignKey(
        QrToken,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="attendances",
    )

    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=STATUS_CHOICES,
        default=ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
    )
    checkin_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    checkout_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    method = models.CharField(
        max_length=30,
        choices=METHOD_CHOICES,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
    )
    recorded_by = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        db_column="recorded_by_user_id",
        related_name="recorded_attendances",
    )

    note = models.TextField(null=True, blank=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "attendance"
        verbose_name = "Attendance"
        verbose_name_plural = "Attendances"
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["event", "user"],
                name="uq_attendance_event_user",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=~Q(status__in=[ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN, ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED])
                | Q(checkin_at__isnull=False),
                name="ck_attendance_checkin",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(checkout_at__isnull=True)
                | (
                    Q(checkin_at__isnull=False)
                    & Q(checkout_at__gte=F("checkin_at"))
                ),
                name="ck_attendance_checkout",
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["event", "status"], name="idx_attend_event_status"),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Attendance: {self.user.display_name} @ {self.event.title} [{self.status}]"

    def clean(self):
        super().clean()
        if self.status in [ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN, ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED] and self.checkin_at is None:
            raise ValidationError({"checkin_at": "checkin_at is required when checked_in or completed."})
        if self.checkout_at is not None:
            if self.checkin_at is None:
                raise ValidationError({"checkout_at": "checkin_at is required if checkout_at is set."})
            if self.checkout_at < self.checkin_at:
                raise ValidationError({"checkout_at": "checkout_at must be >= checkin_at."})


class OnboardingAcknowledgment(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    user = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="onboarding_acknowledgments",
    )
    event = models.ForeignKey(
        "events.Event",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="onboarding_acknowledgments",
    )
    onboarding_version = models.IntegerField()
    method = models.CharField(max_length=30, default="app_tick")
    acknowledged_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "onboarding_acknowledgments"
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["user", "event", "onboarding_version"],
                name="uq_onboarding_ack",
            ),
        ]
        verbose_name = "Onboarding Acknowledgment"
        verbose_name_plural = "Onboarding Acknowledgments"

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.display_name} acknowledged event {self.event_id} (v{self.onboarding_version})"


class Feedback(models.Model):
    DIRECTION_CHOICES = [
        (FEEDBACK_COORDINATOR_TO_VOLUNTEER, "Coordinator to Volunteer"),
        (FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT, "Volunteer to Event"),
        (FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_COORDINATOR, "Volunteer to Coordinator"),
    ]

    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ("active", "Active"),
        ("hidden", "Hidden"),
    ]

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    event = models.ForeignKey(
        "events.Event",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="feedbacks",
    )
    rater_user = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="given_feedbacks",
    )
    target_user = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="received_feedbacks",
    )

    direction = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=DIRECTION_CHOICES)
    score = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
    tags = ChoiceArrayField(
        models.CharField(max_length=50),
        default=list,
        blank=True,
    )
    comment = models.TextField(null=True, blank=True)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default="active")

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "feedback"
        verbose_name = "Feedback"
        verbose_name_plural = "Feedbacks"
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(score__gte=1) & Q(score__lte=5),
                name="ck_feedback_score",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=(
                    Q(direction=FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT) & Q(target_user__isnull=True)
                )
                | (
                    ~Q(direction=FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT) & Q(target_user__isnull=False)
                ),
                name="ck_feedback_target",
            ),
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["event", "rater_user", "direction"],
                condition=Q(target_user__isnull=True),
                name="uq_feedback_event_once",
            ),
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["event", "rater_user", "direction", "target_user"],
                condition=Q(target_user__isnull=False),
                name="uq_feedback_user_once",
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["event"], name="idx_feedback_event"),
            models.Index(fields=["rater_user"], name="idx_feedback_rater"),
            models.Index(fields=["target_user"], name="idx_feedback_target"),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Feedback {self.direction} by {self.rater_user.display_name} ({self.score}/5)"

    def clean(self):
        super().clean()
        if self.score is not None and not (1 <= self.score <= 5):
            raise ValidationError({"score": "score must be between 1 and 5."})
        if self.direction == FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT and self.target_user is not None:
            raise ValidationError({"target_user": "target_user must be null when direction is 'volunteer_to_event'."})
        if self.direction != FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT and self.target_user is None:
            raise ValidationError({"target_user": "target_user is required when direction is not 'volunteer_to_event'."})

# apps/ops/services.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models import Q
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.audit.services import log_action
from apps.common.constants import (
    ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
    ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,
    ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED,
    ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED,
    ATTENDANCE_ABSENT,
    ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN,
    ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED,
    ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
    EVENT_ACTIVE,
    EVENT_COMPLETED,
    FEEDBACK_COORDINATOR_TO_VOLUNTEER,
    FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_COORDINATOR,
    FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT,
    QR_CHECKIN,
    QR_DEFAULT_TTL_MINUTES,
    QR_JOIN_EVENT,
    QR_WALKIN_REGISTRATION,
)
from apps.common.exceptions import (
    CapacityFullError,
    DomainError,
    InvalidStateError,
    OnboardingRequiredError,
    PermissionDeniedError,
)
from apps.common.permissions import (
    ensure_active_account,
    ensure_event_manager,
    is_event_manager,
)
from apps.common.security import generate_raw_token, hash_token
from apps.ops.models import (
    Assignment,
    Attendance,
    Feedback,
    OnboardingAcknowledgment,
    QrToken,
)


def check_post_eligibility(user, post) -> bool:
    """
    Check if a volunteer satisfies post requirements:
    1. If post.required_skills: user.skills must overlap post.required_skills.
    2. If post.physical_level == 'heavy': user.physical_ok and user.can_lift_heavy must be True.
    3. If post.physical_level == 'medium': user.physical_ok must be True.
    4. If post.new_volunteers_allowed is False: user.new_to_volunteer must be False.
    """
    if post.required_skills:
        user_skills = set(user.skills or [])
        required = set(post.required_skills)
        if not user_skills.intersection(required):
            return False

    if post.physical_level == "heavy":
        if not (user.physical_ok and user.can_lift_heavy):
            return False
    elif post.physical_level == "medium":
        if not user.physical_ok:
            return False

    if not post.new_volunteers_allowed and user.new_to_volunteer:
        return False

    return True


def get_remaining_post_capacity(post) -> int:
    active_count = Assignment.objects.filter(
        post=post,
        status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
    ).count()
    return max(0, post.required_headcount - active_count)


def create_assignment_from_application(application, actor=None) -> Assignment | None:
    """
    Create Assignment from an accepted EventApplication.
    - If application already has an active assignment, return it.
    - If application has a specific post, verify post eligibility and capacity.
    - If application has no post, find first eligible open post with capacity.
    - If no eligible post has capacity, return None.
    - Creates Attendance(status='expected') if missing.
    """
    existing = Assignment.objects.filter(
        application=application,
        status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
    ).first()
    if existing:
        return existing

    user = application.user
    event = application.event

    target_post = application.post
    if target_post:
        if target_post.status != "open":
            return None
        if not check_post_eligibility(user, target_post):
            return None
        if get_remaining_post_capacity(target_post) <= 0:
            return None
    else:
        # Search among event's open posts ordered by priority ascending, id ascending
        open_posts = event.posts.filter(status="open").order_by("priority", "id")
        chosen_post = None
        for post in open_posts:
            if check_post_eligibility(user, post) and get_remaining_post_capacity(post) > 0:
                chosen_post = post
                break
        if not chosen_post:
            return None
        target_post = chosen_post

    assigned_by = "auto" if actor is None else ("admin" if getattr(actor, "is_admin", False) else "coordinator")
    assigned_by_user = None if assigned_by == "auto" else actor

    with transaction.atomic():
        assignment = Assignment.objects.create(
            event=event,
            post=target_post,
            user=user,
            application=application,
            status=ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,
            assigned_by=assigned_by,
            assigned_by_user=assigned_by_user,
            schedule_start=event.start_at,
            schedule_end=event.end_at,
            conditions_snapshot=application.conditions_snapshot or {},
        )

        Attendance.objects.get_or_create(
            event=event,
            user=user,
            defaults={
                "assignment": assignment,
                "status": ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
            },
        )

        log_action(
            actor=actor or user,
            action="create_assignment",
            entity_type="assignment",
            entity_id=assignment.id,
            after_data={"post_id": target_post.id if target_post else None, "user_id": user.id},
        )

    return assignment


def generate_qr_token(
    actor,
    event,
    purpose: str,
    max_uses: int = None,
    ttl_minutes: int = 240,
) -> tuple[QrToken, str]:
    """
    Generate QR Token for an event.
    Stores token_hash (SHA-256). Raw token is returned and never persisted.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    valid_purposes = {QR_WALKIN_REGISTRATION, QR_CHECKIN, QR_JOIN_EVENT}
    if purpose not in valid_purposes:
        raise DomainError(f"Invalid QR token purpose: {purpose}", code="invalid_purpose")

    if max_uses is not None and max_uses <= 0:
        raise DomainError("max_uses must be greater than 0.", code="invalid_max_uses")

    raw_token = generate_raw_token()
    token_hash = hash_token(raw_token)

    ttl = ttl_minutes if ttl_minutes is not None else QR_DEFAULT_TTL_MINUTES
    expires_at = timezone.now() + timedelta(minutes=ttl)

    with transaction.atomic():
        qr_token = QrToken.objects.create(
            event=event,
            created_by=actor,
            purpose=purpose,
            token_hash=token_hash,
            max_uses=max_uses,
            expires_at=expires_at,
        )

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="generate_qr_token",
            entity_type="qr_token",
            entity_id=qr_token.id,
            after_data={"purpose": purpose, "max_uses": max_uses, "expires_at": str(expires_at)},
        )

    return qr_token, raw_token


def resolve_qr_token(raw_token: str) -> dict:
    """
    Resolve QR token metadata without consuming it.
    """
    token_hash = hash_token(raw_token)
    qr_token = QrToken.objects.select_related("event").filter(token_hash=token_hash).first()

    if not qr_token:
        raise DomainError("Invalid QR token.", code="invalid_token")

    if qr_token.revoked_at is not None:
        raise DomainError("QR token has been revoked.", code="revoked_token")

    if qr_token.expires_at <= timezone.now():
        raise DomainError("QR token has expired.", code="expired_token")

    if qr_token.max_uses is not None and qr_token.used_count >= qr_token.max_uses:
        raise CapacityFullError("QR token usage limit reached.", code="qr_limit_reached")

    return {
        "event_id": qr_token.event_id,
        "title": qr_token.event.title,
        "purpose": qr_token.purpose,
        "expires_at": qr_token.expires_at,
        "used_count": qr_token.used_count,
        "max_uses": qr_token.max_uses,
    }


def redeem_qr_token(user, raw_token: str) -> dict:
    """
    Safely redeem a QR token within a transaction lock.
    Dispatches according to purpose:
    - walkin_registration -> apply_to_event(source='qr_walkin')
    - checkin -> check_in(method='qr_scan')
    - join_event -> apply_to_event(source='invite')
    """
    ensure_active_account(user)

    token_hash = hash_token(raw_token)

    with transaction.atomic():
        qr_token = (
            QrToken.objects.select_for_update()
            .select_related("event")
            .filter(token_hash=token_hash)
            .first()
        )

        if not qr_token:
            raise DomainError("Invalid QR token.", code="invalid_token")

        if qr_token.revoked_at is not None:
            raise DomainError("QR token has been revoked.", code="revoked_token")

        if qr_token.expires_at <= timezone.now():
            raise DomainError("QR token has expired.", code="expired_token")

        if qr_token.max_uses is not None and qr_token.used_count >= qr_token.max_uses:
            raise CapacityFullError("QR token usage limit reached.", code="qr_limit_reached")

        qr_token.used_count += 1
        qr_token.save(update_fields=["used_count"])

        # Dispatch by purpose
        if qr_token.purpose == QR_WALKIN_REGISTRATION:
            from apps.events.services import apply_to_event
            app = apply_to_event(user, qr_token.event, source="qr_walkin", qr_token=qr_token)
            result = {
                "action": QR_WALKIN_REGISTRATION,
                "application_id": app.id,
                "status": app.status,
            }
        elif qr_token.purpose == QR_CHECKIN:
            att = check_in(
                actor=user,
                event=qr_token.event,
                user=user,
                method="qr_scan",
                qr_token=qr_token,
            )
            result = {
                "action": QR_CHECKIN,
                "status": "checked_in",
                "attendance_id": att.id,
                "checkin_at": att.checkin_at,
            }
        elif qr_token.purpose == QR_JOIN_EVENT:
            from apps.events.services import apply_to_event
            app = apply_to_event(user, qr_token.event, source="invite")
            result = {
                "action": QR_JOIN_EVENT,
                "application_id": app.id,
                "status": app.status,
            }
        else:
            raise DomainError(f"Unsupported purpose: {qr_token.purpose}", code="invalid_purpose")

        log_action(
            actor=user,
            action="redeem_qr_token",
            entity_type="qr_token",
            entity_id=qr_token.id,
            after_data={"purpose": qr_token.purpose, "used_count": qr_token.used_count},
        )

    return result


def acknowledge_onboarding(user, event, method: str = "app_tick") -> OnboardingAcknowledgment:
    """
    Record onboarding acknowledgment for current event onboarding_version.
    """
    ensure_active_account(user)

    with transaction.atomic():
        ack, created = OnboardingAcknowledgment.objects.update_or_create(
            event=event,
            user=user,
            onboarding_version=event.onboarding_version,
            defaults={
                "method": method,
                "acknowledged_at": timezone.now(),
            },
        )

        log_action(
            actor=user,
            action="acknowledge_onboarding",
            entity_type="onboarding_ack",
            entity_id=ack.id,
            after_data={"event_id": event.id, "version": event.onboarding_version, "method": method},
        )

    return ack


def check_in(
    actor,
    event,
    user=None,
    method: str = "qr_scan",
    qr_token=None,
    target_user=None,
) -> Attendance:
    """
    Check-in volunteer to an active event.
    Returns Attendance instance. Idempotent if already checked in.
    """
    ensure_active_account(actor)
    target = user or target_user
    if not target:
        raise DomainError("Target volunteer is required.", code="user_required")

    if event.status != EVENT_ACTIVE:
        raise InvalidStateError(
            f"Event must be active to check in. Current status: '{event.status}'."
        )

    assignment = Assignment.objects.filter(
        event=event,
        user=target,
        status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
    ).first()
    if not assignment:
        raise DomainError(
            "User does not have an active assignment for this event.",
            code="no_active_assignment",
        )

    if event.require_onboarding_ack:
        has_ack = OnboardingAcknowledgment.objects.filter(
            event=event,
            user=target,
            onboarding_version=event.onboarding_version,
        ).exists()
        if not has_ack:
            raise OnboardingRequiredError(
                "Volunteer must acknowledge onboarding requirements before checking in."
            )

    if actor.id == target.id:
        if method != "qr_scan":
            raise DomainError("Volunteers can only check in via QR scan.", code="invalid_checkin_method")
        if not qr_token or qr_token.purpose != QR_CHECKIN:
            raise DomainError("Valid check-in QR token is required.", code="invalid_qr_token")
    else:
        ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    attendance = Attendance.objects.filter(event=event, user=target).first()
    if attendance and attendance.status == ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN:
        return attendance

    now = timezone.now()
    with transaction.atomic():
        if not attendance:
            attendance = Attendance.objects.create(
                event=event,
                user=target,
                assignment=assignment,
                qr_token=qr_token,
                status=ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN,
                checkin_at=now,
                method=method,
                recorded_by=actor,
            )
        else:
            attendance.assignment = assignment
            attendance.qr_token = qr_token
            attendance.status = ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN
            attendance.checkin_at = now
            attendance.method = method
            attendance.recorded_by = actor
            attendance.save()

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="check_in",
            entity_type="attendance",
            entity_id=attendance.id,
            after_data={"method": method, "target_user_id": target.id},
        )

    return attendance


def check_out(actor, event, user=None, target_user=None) -> Attendance:
    """
    Check-out volunteer from an active event.
    """
    ensure_active_account(actor)
    target = user or target_user
    if not target:
        raise DomainError("Target volunteer is required.", code="user_required")

    if actor.id != target.id:
        ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    attendance = Attendance.objects.filter(event=event, user=target).first()
    if not attendance or attendance.status != ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN:
        raise InvalidStateError("Volunteer is not currently checked in.", code="not_checked_in")

    with transaction.atomic():
        attendance.checkout_at = timezone.now()
        attendance.save(update_fields=["checkout_at"])

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="check_out",
            entity_type="attendance",
            entity_id=attendance.id,
            after_data={"checkout_at": str(attendance.checkout_at), "target_user_id": target.id},
        )

    return attendance


def mark_absent(actor, event, user=None, target_user=None) -> Attendance:
    """
    Coordinator or Admin marks volunteer absent.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, event)
    target = user or target_user
    if not target:
        raise DomainError("Target volunteer is required.", code="user_required")

    attendance = Attendance.objects.filter(event=event, user=target).first()
    if attendance and attendance.status in (ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN, ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED):
        raise InvalidStateError(
            f"Cannot mark absent because attendance is already '{attendance.status}'.",
            code="invalid_state",
        )

    assignment = Assignment.objects.filter(
        event=event,
        user=target,
        status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
    ).first()

    with transaction.atomic():
        if not attendance:
            attendance = Attendance.objects.create(
                event=event,
                user=target,
                assignment=assignment,
                status=ATTENDANCE_ABSENT,
                recorded_by=actor,
            )
        else:
            attendance.status = ATTENDANCE_ABSENT
            attendance.recorded_by = actor
            attendance.save(update_fields=["status", "recorded_by"])

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="mark_absent",
            entity_type="attendance",
            entity_id=attendance.id,
            after_data={"target_user_id": target.id},
        )

    return attendance


def submit_feedback(
    user,
    event,
    direction: str,
    target_user=None,
    score: int = None,
    tags=None,
    comment: str = "",
) -> Feedback:
    """
    Submit feedback between coordinator and volunteer or for the event.
    Requirements:
    - Event must be completed.
    - Score must be between 1 and 5.
    - If coordinator_to_volunteer: rater is coordinator/admin, target_user required and had active assignment.
    - If volunteer_to_event: rater had active assignment, target_user must be None.
    - If volunteer_to_coordinator: rater had active assignment, target_user forced to event.coordinator.
    """
    ensure_active_account(user)

    if event.status != EVENT_COMPLETED:
        raise InvalidStateError(
            "Feedback can only be submitted for completed events.",
            code="event_not_completed",
        )

    if score is None or not (1 <= int(score) <= 5):
        raise DomainError("Feedback score is required and must be between 1 and 5.", code="invalid_score")

    valid_directions = {
        FEEDBACK_COORDINATOR_TO_VOLUNTEER,
        FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT,
        FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_COORDINATOR,
    }
    if direction not in valid_directions:
        raise DomainError(f"Invalid feedback direction: {direction}", code="invalid_direction")

    final_target = target_user

    if direction == FEEDBACK_COORDINATOR_TO_VOLUNTEER:
        if not is_event_manager(user, event):
            raise PermissionDeniedError("Only event coordinators or admins can rate volunteers.")
        if not final_target:
            raise DomainError("Target volunteer is required.", code="target_user_required")
        had_assignment = Assignment.objects.filter(
            event=event,
            user=final_target,
        ).exclude(status=ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED).exists()
        if not had_assignment:
            raise DomainError("Target volunteer had no assignment for this event.", code="invalid_target_user")

    elif direction == FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT:
        had_assignment = Assignment.objects.filter(
            event=event,
            user=user,
        ).exclude(status=ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED).exists()
        if not had_assignment:
            raise PermissionDeniedError("Only participating volunteers can submit event feedback.")
        final_target = None

    elif direction == FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_COORDINATOR:
        had_assignment = Assignment.objects.filter(
            event=event,
            user=user,
        ).exclude(status=ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED).exists()
        if not had_assignment:
            raise PermissionDeniedError("Only participating volunteers can submit coordinator feedback.")
        final_target = event.coordinator

    # Check duplicate feedback
    if final_target is None:
        if Feedback.objects.filter(event=event, rater_user=user, direction=direction, target_user__isnull=True).exists():
            raise DomainError("Feedback has already been submitted for this event.", code="duplicate_feedback")
    else:
        if Feedback.objects.filter(event=event, rater_user=user, direction=direction, target_user=final_target).exists():
            raise DomainError("Feedback has already been submitted for this user.", code="duplicate_feedback")

    with transaction.atomic():
        feedback = Feedback.objects.create(
            event=event,
            rater_user=user,
            target_user=final_target,
            direction=direction,
            score=int(score),
            tags=tags or [],
            comment=comment or "",
            status="active",
        )

        log_action(
            actor=user,
            action="submit_feedback",
            entity_type="feedback",
            entity_id=feedback.id,
            after_data={
                "direction": direction,
                "score": int(score),
                "target_user_id": final_target.id if final_target else None,
            },
        )

    return feedback


def run_auto_assignment(actor, event) -> list[Assignment]:
    """
    Coordinator or Admin runs automated assignment algorithm.
    1. Lock event.
    2. Get accepted applications without active assignment.
    3. For each application, call create_assignment_from_application.
    4. Build groups: group by post, group size = event.group_size. Code format "G1", "G2" per post.
    5. Leader assignment: if post.leader_needed or event.leader_strategy in ("first_experienced", "experienced_preferred"),
       choose first member with has_leader_experience=True. Ensure only one leader per group.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, event)

    from apps.events.models import Event, EventApplication, EventPost

    created_assignments = []

    with transaction.atomic():
        locked_event = Event.objects.select_for_update().get(id=event.id)

        # Get accepted applications without active assignment
        unassigned_apps = (
            EventApplication.objects.select_for_update()
            .filter(event=locked_event, status="accepted")
            .filter(~Q(assignments__status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES))
            .distinct()
            .order_by("applied_at", "id")
        )

        for app in unassigned_apps:
            assignment = create_assignment_from_application(app, actor=None)
            if assignment:
                created_assignments.append(assignment)

        # Group organization and leader assignment
        group_size = locked_event.group_size
        posts = locked_event.posts.all().order_by("priority", "id")

        group_seq = 1
        for post in posts:
            post_assignments = list(
                Assignment.objects.select_for_update()
                .filter(event=locked_event, post=post, status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES)
                .select_related("user")
                .order_by("id")
            )

            if not post_assignments:
                continue

            chunk_size = group_size if (group_size and group_size > 0) else len(post_assignments)

            for i in range(0, len(post_assignments), chunk_size):
                chunk = post_assignments[i : i + chunk_size]
                group_code = f"G{group_seq}"
                group_seq += 1

                # Check if group already has an active leader
                has_existing_leader = any(a.is_group_leader for a in chunk)
                leader_assigned = has_existing_leader

                should_assign_leader = post.leader_needed or locked_event.leader_strategy in (
                    "first_experienced",
                    "experienced_preferred",
                )

                for a in chunk:
                    a.group_code = group_code
                    if should_assign_leader and not leader_assigned and a.user.has_leader_experience:
                        a.is_group_leader = True
                        leader_assigned = True
                    elif not has_existing_leader and not a.is_group_leader:
                        a.is_group_leader = False
                    a.save(update_fields=["group_code", "is_group_leader"])

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="run_auto_assignment",
            entity_type="event",
            entity_id=locked_event.id,
            after_data={"assignments_created": len(created_assignments)},
        )

    return created_assignments


def assign_manually(
    actor,
    application,
    post=None,
    group_code=None,
    is_group_leader: bool = False,
) -> Assignment:
    """
    Coordinator or Admin manually assigns or updates volunteer assignment.
    """
    ensure_event_manager(actor, application.event)

    target_post = post or application.post

    with transaction.atomic():
        if application.status != "accepted":
            application.status = "accepted"
            application.decided_by = actor
            application.decided_at = timezone.now()
            if target_post:
                application.post = target_post
            application.save()

        # Check existing active assignment
        assignment = Assignment.objects.filter(
            application=application,
            status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES,
        ).first()

        if is_group_leader and not group_code:
            raise DomainError(
                "Group leader must have a group code assigned.",
                code="group_code_required",
            )

        if is_group_leader and group_code:
            existing_leader = (
                Assignment.objects.filter(
                    event=application.event,
                    group_code=group_code,
                    is_group_leader=True,
                )
                .exclude(id=assignment.id if assignment else None)
                .filter(status__in=ACTIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES)
                .exists()
            )
            if existing_leader:
                raise DomainError(
                    f"Group '{group_code}' already has an active leader.",
                    code="duplicate_group_leader",
                )

        assigned_by = "admin" if getattr(actor, "is_admin", False) else "coordinator"

        if not assignment:
            assignment = Assignment.objects.create(
                event=application.event,
                post=target_post,
                user=application.user,
                application=application,
                status=ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,
                assigned_by=assigned_by,
                assigned_by_user=actor,
                group_code=group_code,
                is_group_leader=is_group_leader,
                schedule_start=application.event.start_at,
                schedule_end=application.event.end_at,
                conditions_snapshot=application.conditions_snapshot or {},
            )
            Attendance.objects.get_or_create(
                event=application.event,
                user=application.user,
                defaults={
                    "assignment": assignment,
                    "status": ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
                },
            )
        else:
            assignment.post = target_post
            assignment.group_code = group_code
            assignment.is_group_leader = is_group_leader
            assignment.assigned_by = assigned_by
            assignment.assigned_by_user = actor
            assignment.save()

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="assign_manually",
            entity_type="assignment",
            entity_id=assignment.id,
            after_data={"post_id": target_post.id if target_post else None, "group_code": group_code},
        )

    return assignment


def confirm_assignment(user, assignment: Assignment) -> Assignment:
    """
    Volunteer confirms own assignment.
    """
    if assignment.user_id != user.id:
        raise PermissionDeniedError("Cannot confirm another volunteer's assignment.")

    if assignment.status not in (ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,):
        if assignment.status == ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED:
            return assignment
        raise InvalidStateError(f"Cannot confirm assignment in status '{assignment.status}'.")

    with transaction.atomic():
        assignment.status = ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED
        assignment.save(update_fields=["status"])

        log_action(
            actor=user,
            action="confirm_assignment",
            entity_type="assignment",
            entity_id=assignment.id,
        )

    return assignment


def cancel_assignment(actor, assignment: Assignment, reason: str = "") -> Assignment:
    """
    Coordinator, Admin, or Volunteer cancels an assignment.
    """
    if assignment.user_id != actor.id:
        ensure_event_manager(actor, assignment.event)

    if assignment.status == ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED:
        return assignment

    with transaction.atomic():
        assignment.status = ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED
        assignment.save(update_fields=["status"])

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="cancel_assignment",
            entity_type="assignment",
            entity_id=assignment.id,
            after_data={"reason": reason},
        )

    return assignment
